# Kabso Live — AI Workforce Board

Authoritative task registry. Statuses: `BACKLOG → READY → IN_PROGRESS → REVIEW → TESTING → DONE`
(+ `BLOCKED`, `REJECTED`). A task is DONE only when acceptance criteria are met, its tests were
actually executed, and required reviews passed. Delivery labels used in reports:
**IMPLEMENTED** (code exists) ≠ **TESTED** (tests executed and passing) ≠ **INTEGRATED** (merged
on the integration branch with the other modules, regression suite green) ≠ **PRODUCTION-READY**
(release gates passed + human authorization).

## Phase 1 integration gate — PASSED 2026-10-16 (internal milestone)

Gate commit **`3b80e0a`**: CI run 29 attempt 2 all 8 jobs success (each job result verified against
the success|skipped allowlist), QA KL-006 **approve**, Security KL-007 **pass with conditions**.
Report: `docs/PHASE1_REPORT.md`. Production readiness: **no** — see `docs/PRE_PRODUCTION_CHECKLIST.md`.
`main` not yet created: pushing it was blocked by this session's permission policy (owner action).

Post-gate integrated: contract v1.2 (`3ec65ca`; API unit 203/203, e2e 147/147, Flutter 347/347,
CI run 36 green) — QA/Security re-test of v1.2 pending; v1.2.1 follow-up (KL-017b) in progress.

**Closed defects:** D1 (gitleaks fixtures) — `e424d77`; KL-010-D1 (default-branch concurrency) — `a04201b`; KL-011-D1 (Argon2 cap: lazy dummy hash outside the limiter → uncapped work + first-call timing side channel; flaky test) — `0944980`.

**Repository facts (2026-10-15):** visibility **public**; default branch `claude/amazing-tesla-5aj3um`
(no `main`), so the iOS CI job runs only on `workflow_dispatch`. Owner decisions pending:
visibility, creating `main` as default, Dependabot PRs #1/#2, B9 sign-off, KL-013 deferral.

**Watch items:** `material_ui` 1.7.0 (publisher flutter.dev, required by go_router 18) was
published 2026-10-14 — pinned by `pubspec.lock`, CI now uses `--enforce-lockfile`.
**Incident:** A7 killed a `node dist/main.js` process it did not start (PID 16769, probably QA's
server) using a pattern kill; QA notified to re-run affected checks. Agents must stop processes
by recorded PID only.

### Acceptance criteria

**KL-009 Accessibility token fixes** — Approved scope: A5 proposals P1 (dark `textTertiary` →
#92929E), P2 (light `textTertiary` → #666671), P3/P4 (add `primaryText` dark #FF3B47 / light
#C8080F), P5 (light `success` → #137A43), P6 (light `warning` → #955C00), P7 (light `error` →
#C4242D), P8 (light `info` → #1A64BF), P9 (add `borderStrong` dark #70707C / light #85858F),
P11 (add `focusRing`: dark red400, light `primary`), P14 (add `onStatus` #0B0B0D). Process:
(1) A2 + A5 review the proposals against the merged Flutter token implementation and list every
usage site affected; (2) CTO records the contract change (design-tokens v1.1) with compatibility
notes; (3) A2 applies the change to `design-tokens.json` and Flutter tokens/theme in one change
set and switches text-on-red, input outlines, focus indicators and text-on-status-fill to the new
roles; (4) a Flutter test asserts WCAG ratios ≥ 4.5:1 for body-text pairs and ≥ 3:1 for
outlines/large text, computed from the token constants; (5) `flutter analyze`/`flutter test`
green. P10, P12, P13, P15–P19 remain proposals (not approved).


**KL-002 Backend auth slice** — every endpoint in `docs/contracts/openapi.yaml` implemented with
the exact shapes and error codes of `CONVENTIONS.md`; Prisma schema + initial migration
(User, UserProfile, UserSettings, Session, VerificationToken, AuditLog); Argon2id; refresh
rotation with reuse detection; immediate revocation; Redis rate limits; log mail adapter;
health endpoints; zod-validated config; e2e tests against real Postgres+Redis covering happy
paths, validation, duplicate email/username, wrong password (no enumeration), refresh rotation,
reuse detection, logout, session revoke, suspended account, password reset revoking sessions,
rate limiting; lint + typecheck + tests green.

**KL-003 Flutter slice** — app boots to splash → auth gate; Arabic default (RTL) + English;
dark (default) + light themes from `design-tokens.json`; 5-tab `StatefulShellRoute` with
honest "coming in Phase N" empty states (no fake data, no dead buttons); login, register (live
username availability), forgot-password, email-verification notice screens wired to the API;
Dio client with error-envelope mapping and single-flight refresh; refresh token in secure
storage; session restore on launch; logout; loading/error/empty states; unit tests (repository,
interceptor refresh single-flight, error mapping), widget tests (login validation, RTL layout,
shell navigation); `dart format`, `flutter analyze` (zero issues), `flutter test` green.

**KL-004 Design spec** — `docs/design/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (tokens usage, components with states,
RTL rules, accessibility, motion) and `docs/design/SCREEN_INVENTORY.md` (all screens across
phases, navigation map, per-screen states) consistent with `design-tokens.json`.

**KL-005 DevOps** — `docker-compose.yml` (Postgres 16, Redis 7, MinIO), `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
(api: install, prisma generate, migrate, lint, typecheck, test with service containers;
mobile: pub get, codegen-diff check, format check, analyze, test; contracts: OpenAPI lint),
`docs/DEPLOYMENT.md`, root `.env.example` guidance.

## Decision log

| # | Date | Decision | Rationale |
| - | ---- | -------- | --------- |
| D1 | 2026-10-15 | Monorepo: `apps/mobile`, `apps/api`, `docs/contracts` | Contract-first, one review surface |
| D2 | 2026-10-15 | NestJS 12 + Prisma 7.10 (stable; npm `latest` is 8.0.0-rc → rejected) + Postgres 16 + Redis | Stable versions only |
| D3 | 2026-10-15 | LiveKit as default live provider behind `LiveMediaProvider` | OSS/self-hostable, Flutter SDK, multi-host |
| D4 | 2026-10-15 | Opaque rotating refresh tokens `<sid>.<secret>` + 15-min JWT; guard checks session | Immediate revocation, reuse detection |
| D5 | 2026-10-15 | Generated Dart code committed, CI checks drift | Analyzer works on fresh clone |
| D6 | 2026-10-15 | Error messages localized client-side by `code` | Single source of copy in the app |
| D7 | 2026-10-15 | OpenAPI v1.0.1/v1.0.2: YAML fix + additive documentation of 400/429 responses; health 4xx warnings deferred | No shape change; see CONVENTIONS §9 |
| D9 | 2026-10-15 | Contract v1.0.3 ratifies KL-002 clarifications (issue vocabulary, 400 not 413/415, suspended token → 401, extra limits, revokeSession 404) | Behaviour already implemented and tested; A2 notified |
| D10 | 2026-10-15 | Backend pins TypeScript 6.0.3 (not 7.0.2) and Vitest+SWC (not Jest) | TS 7 lacks the compiler API typescript-eslint/@nestjs/cli need; Nest 12 is ESM-only |
| D11 | 2026-10-15 | Mobile uses `material_ui` (decoupled Material, flutter.dev) instead of `flutter/material.dart`; Dart 3.13 `new(...)` constructor syntax | go_router 18 requires it; single theme/localizations source |
| D12 | 2026-10-15 | Contract v1.0.4: ARGB token hex, explicit username length check, B7 placeholder id corrected | Documentation only |
| D13 | 2026-10-15 | Contract v1.1 adopts KL-007 recommendations (see CONVENTIONS §9); EMAIL_TAKEN kept for Phase 1 as accepted risk → launch blocker B9 | Security gate conditions; no shape change |

| D63 | 2026-10-19 | **Owner: the launch animation "Kabso splash".** The owner's Lottie (Kabi hops in, 1080×1920, 180 frames at 60 fps) and voice ("kab kab kabsooo") replace the wordmark-and-spinner splash. The router keeps `/splash` until the animation and its 350 ms hold have played (`splashIntroProvider`), then applies the auth redirect as before; a failed restore shows its error state after the animation, a slow one a spinner. The "kabso." wordmark uses Fredoka Bold bundled in the app (OFL, `assets/fonts/OFL-Fredoka.txt`), not `google_fonts` at runtime. The voice plays once per cold start and mixes with the user's audio (iOS `ambient`, silenced by the ring/silent switch; Android media usage without audio focus, so it follows the media volume); browsers block it. *Fix (2026-10-26, owner device report: no voice):* `splashVoiceProvider` was `Provider.autoDispose`, so it was disposed right after the splash read it and released the player before the voice was heard; it is now a plain `Provider`, and the voice owns its player until playback completes. It plays with `PlayerMode.mediaPlayer` (the low-latency SoundPool mode reports no completion and decodes MP3 unreliably on some Android devices) on the media stream instead of the sonification stream many phones keep muted (`splash_voice_test.dart`). Reduced motion shows the last frame without the voice. Native launch screens use the animation's background (#0F0F0F) so there is no white flash. New dependencies: `lottie`, `audioplayers`. *Replaced by D156 (2026-10-28): the owner's launch video; the voice is removed, the Lottie stays as the welcome screen's Kabi.* | Owner-supplied assets; measured against the owner's preview video (SSIM 0.994 on the last frame) |

Kabi stands on the seam and follows it (700 ms); small gains: push toward the line's motion (mirrored to the left), back to idle after 1.3 s; a team gaining ≥ 6 % of the total within 3 s: fire on Kabi and on that team's side for 2.6 s; last 10 s: tense; result: a jump toward the winner (draw: a wave); idle: blinks, and a wave or hop every 5–10 s. The owner confirmed the art and its sound are theirs / licensed. *Owner values for the gift:* code `angel_warrior`, الملاك المحارب / Angel Warrior, **50 coins**, tab **FUN** (مرح), tag **luxury** (فخامة / Luxury), and the gift's **sound must play with the animation**. *Contract-first (v1.26.2-live draft, CONVENTIONS §10.23):* `Gift` (so `GiftBagItem`) and `LiveGift` gain `imageUrl`, `animationUrl` (public URLs under `MEDIA_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`) and `animationBytes`, required in responses and null when absent; `GiftCatalogue.version` moves with them; the bundled `assetKey` / `animationKey` stay the fallback. Migration `20261119090000_gift_art` (nullable `image_object_key`, `animation_object_key`, `animation_bytes`; CHECKs on the key formats and on the size travelling with the SVGA). *CLI:* `gift-admin add|update … --image <file.png|webp> --animation-file <file.svga>` checks the bytes (PNG/WebP magic, ≤ 1 MB, 64–2048 px, at most 5:4; SVGA 2.x zlib stream that inflates to a MovieEntity, ≤ 25 MB) before anything is uploaded or changed, uploads to content-addressed immutable keys `media/gifts/<code>/<first 32 hex of SHA-256>.<ext>` (`image/png` / `image/webp` / `application/octet-stream`, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`) with the existing S3 adapter, then stores the keys and size (audited); `--image none` / `--animation-file none` clear them; `list` shows them. Runs in the `migrate` job with the files mounted (`-v /root/gifts:/gifts:ro`) and the API's `S3_*` settings passed with `-e` (DEPLOYMENT §5.7). *App:* the panel, the banner and the effect show `imageUrl` (downloaded once into a disk cache, `GiftArtCache`, keyed by the URL's SHA-256, 300 MB with oldest-used eviction), else the bundled image, else Kabso's gift box; the effect plays `animationUrl` (else the bundled SVGA) full screen, once, from the cached bytes, while the image pops; a download that fails or takes more than 6 s skips the animation for that send (the download continues for the next); the panel prefetches animations ≤ 2 MB when it opens, larger ones download when first sent or received; decoded frames and the sound are released when the effect ends; the effect holds the screen until the SVGA ends (at most 30 s) instead of the image's 3.2 s. *Sound:* the SVGA carries two MP3 clips (frames 0–152 and 149–300, 87 KB each); the app plays them itself at their frames (not the SVGA player's audio, which is cleared): Android media stream without taking audio focus, so the live's audio and other apps keep playing and the media volume applies; iOS in the session the live already set (no category change, which could disturb the live microphone; so the silent switch does not mute it, like the live's own audio); never while the app is in the background; stopped when the effect ends or the app leaves the foreground. There is no in-app mute rule yet. *CTO choices (owner may change):* the rows keep object keys and the API builds URLs from `MEDIA_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` (a CDN domain change needs no data change); SVGA 1.x zip files are refused (the app's player reads 2.x only); replaced files stay in the bucket; badge **NEW** and sort 10 for this gift (not stated by the owner). The SVGA decodes in 0.46 s in a widget test: 2.1.0, 750 × 1624, 30 fps, 300 frames (10 s), 125 bitmaps (~142 MB decoded while it plays). The files are not in git (D40). | Owner (chat, 2026-10-26); applied (migration `20261119090000_gift_art`; e2e kl081, kl089; app tests `gift_art_test`). |

| D132 | 2026-10-27 | **Owner: real lucky gifts (option 2: random coin wins), RTP 85 %, multipliers ×5 … ×500, host share 10 %, daily protection ("the app never loses"); publish the odds, 18+, store rules, regions (`LUCKY_DECISIONS.md`).** *Contract-first (v1.27-live draft, CONVENTIONS §10.27):* gift category **`LUCKY`** (listed only while `LUCKY_GIFTS_ENABLED`; old apps drop unknown categories in the catalogue and the bag and ignore unknown fields / data messages, so **no crash and no forced update**; using lucky gifts needs the app update); `LiveGiftResult.luckyResult` `{draws, wins[{multiplier,count,coins}], coinsWon, topMultiplier, downgraded}` (LUCKY sends only); data message `lucky-win` on topic `lucky` when the best win ≥ ×100; `GET /live/gifts/lucky/odds` (guests too; `caller.eligible/reason`); `PUT /me/lucky-gifts/age-confirmation` `{adult:true}`; 403 `LUCKY_GIFT_AGE_UNCONFIRMED`, `LUCKY_GIFT_REGION_UNAVAILABLE`; ledger `LUCKY_GIFT` / `LUCKY_GIFT_WIN`. *Mechanics (CTO):* one draw per gift (`quantity × recipients`), `crypto.randomInt` over 10^6; chance of `m_i` = `floor(RTP_bps × share_i / m_i)` per million with configured shares 40/25/15/12/8 → ×5 6.8 %, ×10 2.125 %, ×50 0.255 %, ×100 0.102 %, ×500 0.0136 %, no win 90.7044 %, Σp·m = 85.000 % exactly; daily protection per day (UTC, configurable): wins paid in draw order while `paid ≤ floor(spend × RTP)` — a draw that does not fit is downgraded to the largest multiplier that fits or withheld (`downgraded`). Charge, day row, win credit, audit row (`lucky_gift_draws`: every winning draw, table, budget before/after) and crediting in the send's transaction under the existing `clientId` idempotency (a retry never draws). *Crediting:* the room score, popularity board, Contribution, recipients' charm and family points count **10 %** (`C = floor(V × 10 %)`, score 100 × C); the sender's **Wizard** counts the full value (CP-005); Support only if the SUPPORT version's `luckyGiftsCountAsSupport`; winnings count for nothing. Lucky gifts are COINS only (bag → 400 `source`; `gift-admin grant` refuses). Config `LUCKY_GIFT_*` (env; invalid → refuse to start; RTP + host share ≤ 100 %); region list matched against the edge country header (fail closed) and `Me.country`. *Pending owner / legal review before switching on:* the odds, the 18+ self-declaration (no birth date exists), the country list, store loot-box rules. Admin: `gift-admin lucky-wins`, `lucky-day`. Migrations `20261127090000_lucky_gifts_enums`, `20261127100000_lucky_gifts`. | Owner (chat, 2026-10-27); unit `lucky-odds.spec`; e2e `kl093-lucky-gifts` |

| D135 | 2026-10-27 | **Owner feedback from the real phone (staging build of d5cf55e).** (1) «خلفية عند المضيف يغيرها في الغرفة صوتية لا يظهر التغير عند الاخرين»: *root causes* — the API changed `theme` in `PATCH /live/rooms/{id}` without moving any version (`LiveSeatsService.updateSettings`), and the viewer screen drew the room it opened with (`_RoomView` used `widget.room`; the session's re-read `room` after `roomInfoVersion` was never shown — so a topic or title edit did not show either). *Fix, contract-first (v1.27.2-live, CONVENTIONS §10.9, §10.12):* a real theme change moves the room info version (`roomInfoVersion` in the media room's metadata, written at once); the same theme again changes nothing; the viewer draws the last room read. (2) «الدعم الذي تلقاه الاخرون لا يظهر تحت المايك»: only the host tile had points (the live score); nothing gave a seated person's received points. *Contract (v1.27.2-live):* `LiveSeat.points` (required) — the gift points the seated person received in this live, 100 a counted coin as the live score counts gifts (a LUCKY gift its host share, from its stored draw), 0 for empty and locked seats; a gift naming someone seated (other than the host) raises the seat `version` in the send's transaction and the metadata follows with the score's write (≤ 2 s), so every app re-reads the seats. *App:* every taken audio seat shows the cherry pill under the name (the host tile keeps the live score). (2) **Rules line order (D135 final):** on the device the lines sat *above* the rules line, which stayed at the bottom. *Root causes:* the chat controller (autoDispose) is rebuilt when the host's live is minimized and reopened — the new one put the rules line after the whole history (now holding every line said since), and a line that arrived while the history loaded was pulled above it by its copy in the history. *Fix:* `liveChatVisitsProvider` remembers after which history message the rules line was said for each room in this app session (the viewer screen forgets it on leaving: `endLiveChatVisit`), and lines already shown win over their history copy; so it stays the first line of the visit, rises with the messages and leaves the top. (5) **One header gap:** `KMetrics.liveHeaderGap` = 10 pt — the visible top of the video host pill and of the audio room card are both 10 pt under the status bar (host and viewer, solo and multi; test compares them). Promo artwork for the owner (Kabso art only: family podium frame and emblem, the family hero, Readex Pro, brand red/gold), 600 × 800 PNG + WebP, outside git. (2) **Engine:** `LiveMediaEngine` gains `filtersSupported` (web, and Android with the plugin) and `arEffects` (`LiveArSupport.unsupported/unlicensed/ready`); `LiveCameraFx` attaches after `camera.start()` and **re-attaches after unmute** (LiveKit restarts the track with a new id), releases in `_stopVideo`; never in audio rooms (no camera). `LiveBeauty` carries `effect` (`LiveArEffect.none/backgroundBlur`) beside `filter`; Beauty (skin) stays web-only (`beautySupported`). | D144a | 2026-10-28 | **Owner device report: DeepAR «Background blur» freezes the picture on a still (DeepAR's watermark showed, so licence, initialization and the first render worked; colour filters work; None recovers) — plus the camera/microphone permission UX.** *Root cause (code):* D144's `DeepArStage` published whatever render was waiting in its one-deep slot as the current frame's output (`DeepArStage.kt` D144 l.276–287 with `Slot.take` l.404–414), never asking whether DeepAR had taken a newer camera frame. DeepAR in live mode re-draws its last processed image into the render surface, so a render was nearly always waiting; once DeepAR stopped taking input the stage kept publishing the same picture (with the watermark) — a freeze, not the raw-camera fallback its comment promised. Contributing triggers in the same code: no input geometry latch (each WebRTC resolution change — CPU/bandwidth adaptation, often in the first seconds — fed DeepAR a new size and rebuilt the render surface, l.253–262/290–302, disposing the old surface *before* `setRenderSurface` of the new one, l.213–227; DeepAR keys its pipeline to the first input, its own Flutter plugin froze for good on a mid-stream rotation change), an unbounded `receiveFrame` backlog on DeepAR's thread (one post per camera frame, l.266, no in-flight gate), and the camera flip (rotation 270↔90) on the same instance. Bytecode facts used (DeepAR 5.6.22): `receiveFrame` silently drops frames unless `engineState==2 && !isPaused`; `setPaused` only touches the native side; `initialized()` arrives on the main looper; the thread that first calls `setRenderSurface` owns DeepAR. *Fix (`packages/kabso_camera_fx`):* (1) `FreshGate` (`FrameGate.kt`): every feed has a sequence number (`feedStarted` before `receiveFrame`, `feedDone` after); a render is published only if its tag is newer than the last published one **and** that feed is done — re-draws are released at once (the helper's single texture comes back), a hung DeepAR thread never publishes its re-draws; no fresh render in 40 ms → the raw frame. (2) One feed in flight (busy → wait for the in-flight render, no queue). (3) `InputGeometry`: DeepAR's input latched at the first frame (long side ≤ 1280); other sizes GPU-cropped/scaled to it before the readback, the output scaled back to what WebRTC sent; the render surface set once (new before old); a rotation change (other camera) → a new DeepAR instance. (4) Safety net `OutputHealth`: 10 s warm-up to the first fresh render, then 30 misses in a row → the effect turns itself off (raw camera), a `receiveFrame` exception, a 6 s stall, a licence error or a failed start/surface likewise; teardown releases DeepAR before its surface. (5) Status to Dart on `com.kabso/camera_fx` (`status` `{state: off|initializing|ready|licence_failed|error|disabled, reason}`), `diagnostics` for the overlay (never the key, only `licencePresent`). *App:* `LiveMediaEngine.arStatus`/`arDiagnostics`; the host controller switches the effect back to None when DeepAR gives up; Magic shows None selected and «المؤثر غير متاح الآن: …» (stopped / licence / did not start) or «جارٍ تشغيل المؤثر…»; **diagnostics overlay** (`LiveFxDiagnostics`, debug/profile only — `!kReleaseMode` — video lives with the processor): state+reason, licence yes/no, frames in/fed/out/raw/consecutive misses, DeepAR renders/delivered/stale/busy, avg wait and readback ms, in-flight ms, latched input, instances, SDK, last error; tap folds it. *Permissions:* `KabsoPermissions` (channel `com.kabso/permissions`, Android: granted / notAsked / denied / blocked — first request remembered in prefs, `ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS`); app `LivePermissions` port: go-live and seat requests check first — granted: start; notAsked/denied: a rationale sheet (`showLivePermissionSheet`) **then** the system prompt once (Not now asks nothing); blocked: the notice/sheet with «فتح الإعدادات», and the camera starts on return from the settings (app resumed). Audio rooms and audio seats ask for the **microphone only** (verified: LiveKit's audio track asks `getUserMedia({audio, video:false})`, flutter_webrtc requests `RECORD_AUDIO` only; the audio path never creates a camera track). Elsewhere (iOS, web) `unknown`: the capture asks itself, as before. Manifest: `CAMERA`, `RECORD_AUDIO` declared; iOS `NSCameraUsageDescription`/`NSMicrophoneUsageDescription` present, English only (no `ar.lproj/InfoPlist.strings`; adding one needs the Xcode project — open). *Not verified here (no device):* that the freeze is gone on the owner's phone, DeepAR's real render cadence in live mode, fps/latency with the gate, the camera-flip re-initialization, the permission dialogs on Android 11–15. (2) **«التسجيل / Recorder» — «احفظ آخر 60 ثانية» (item 11), design chosen:** **server-side LiveKit Egress**, not an on-device ring buffer — it records what viewers see (the room composite, gifts and guests included, audio rooms too) for every host and phone (iOS cannot record a WebRTC track from Dart; a device buffer gets only the host's own camera and costs battery and storage), and the 🔴 indicator follows the egress state on the server. Flow: the host turns the buffer on → `RoomCompositeEgress` to segmented HLS (6 s segments) in object storage, the last ~90 s kept → every one in the room gets a 🔴 data message (seated guests are told; guests' consent per room policy) → «احفظ آخر 60 ثانية» makes the server join the last 10 segments (ffmpeg, no re-encode) into an MP4 in the media store and opens a post draft (M1) for the host to caption → 20 clips a day per host; the buffer stops when the host turns it off or the live ends. Cost: about one CPU core per 720p composite on the LiveKit box. **Blocked** by infrastructure not available here: the `livekit-egress` service and its storage bucket/credentials. (3) **«كاميرا مزدوجة / Dual camera» (item 12):** needs concurrent front+back capture (Android 11+ `CameraManager.getConcurrentCameraIds` / CameraX concurrent cameras; iOS `AVCaptureMultiCamSession.isMultiCamSupported`) composed picture-in-picture into the published track natively — native code not verifiable here; with no detection the tile stays «قريباً» (owner: shown only on supporting phones once built). (2) `FramePath`: at attach the plugin finds the track's `VideoSource` (flutter_webrtc's private `methodCallHandler → getUserMediaImpl → mVideoCapturers`, kept by its consumer ProGuard rules) and, if the track is not its processor, `setVideoProcessor(track)`; any mismatch → `unknown`, nothing changed. (5) App: `LiveCameraGate` — a camera stop is registered synchronously (`stop()` before leaving the room) and a camera start waits for it (≤ 4 s); `startPreview` is single-flight per engine; `stop()` always stops the camera (`try/finally`). | D144c | 2026-10-29 | **Owner device test of staging 739fa7c (D144b + D160; screenshots 228, 229, scratch only). D144b's frame path held (`camera` rising, `path source`). New: (A, 228) `DeepAR error (init_timeout) / in 301 fed 0 out 0 raw 301 … init no #1 on track / camera 654 path source`; (B, 229, the attempt before it) `disabled (black_output) … luma in 138 out 136 black 2.0 s probe passthrough_ok … init yes … WARNING: Switch effect called but DeepAR rendering is paused!`; (C) «تمويه الخلفية لا تعمل».** *What DeepAR 5.6.22 does (javap of `classes.jar`; `llvm-objdump` of arm64 `libnative-lib.so`; scratch only):* the native engine is **one per process**: `Java_…_DeepAR_isInitialized` reads one global flag, which `Java_…_DeepAR_shutdown` clears only at its end (0x24f4a0). `DeepAR.initialize()` starts nothing: the engine starts in `setRenderSurface` when `engineState == 0`, **unless that flag is still set**. In that case it re-posts itself to the **main looper** every 150 ms (`DeepAR$1`), for ever, and `initialized()` never comes. `Controller::switchEffect` (0x2781f4) checks the paused byte that `Controller::pause` sets (0x2780b8), logs «Switch effect called but DeepAR rendering is paused!» and **returns without switching**. *(A), most likely cause (no device log):* a DeepAR from an earlier go-live in the same process was not released yet, so the new one stayed in the re-post loop. It matches 228 coming right after 229, whose DeepAR the stage had **paused** (D144a `fail(disabled)` → `pauseDeepAr`). Whether releasing a paused engine hangs on the device is **not proven**. *(B):* the warning is our own sequence after the black stop: pause, then None → `switchEffect` on a paused engine. It is a consequence, not the black's cause; it also overwrote the black-output error line. The black itself: DeepAR without the effect drew the camera (`passthrough_ok`), so the blur's render was black **for the 2 s D145e allowed before stopping**; meanwhile D145e had already published those black frames. The leading explanation is DeepAR's segmentation still loading (`DeepARVision::initializeSegmentationAsync`; the AAR's `models.bin` is 17 MB). It is **not proven**. *(C):* blur is DeepAR's built-in `backgroundBlur(true, 6)`, which loads the SDK's own `assets/background_blur.deepar` (merged from the AAR; not ours). It "did not work" because of (A) and (B), not because of a separate defect found here. *Fix (`kabso_camera_fx`):* (1) `EngineSlot` (process-wide): a stage starts its DeepAR only after the previous one's `release()` has returned. It waits by re-posting every 200 ms; if still waiting at 10 s, the effect stops with `error (engine_busy)` and Magic says it did not start. (2) **Never paused:** None and every failure release DeepAR (`teardown`) instead of `setPaused(true)`; `setPaused` is no longer called at all. (3) `OutputVerifier`: a render goes out only once one has been read back lit. Before that, every render is read back; afterwards, every 8th. A black render (camera lit, output ≤ 28) sends the camera instead and goes back to checking every render. Only 8 s of black in a row gives up (`disabled (black_output)`, then the D145e probe). A read-back that fails, or a dark camera, cannot be judged, so the render goes out. (4) Diagnostics and overlay: `engine` (DeepAR's private `engineState` read by reflection, kept by the plugin's ProGuard rule), `lit N ms` (from the pick to the first lit picture), `held N` (black renders replaced), and DeepAR's warnings on their own `warn` line (`err` keeps the error). Plus `StartWatchTest` 13, `FrameGateTest` 13, `BlackOutputTest` 9 = 43/43 in the scratch probe app, which also compiled the plugin's Kotlin against DeepAR 5.6.22 / WebRTC 150.7871.01 / flutter_webrtc 1.6.2+hotfix.4. Strength: a diagonal gradient from each badge's colour (≈ 90 % white at the top start, 72 % in the middle, a white sheen low, 45 % at the bottom end corner — M on the board's eight cards), a white edge and a tinted shadow; locked tiers desaturated 60 %, still legible. The header is the same card full width (the level, «تحتاج X خبرة…», the bar, the points, «الحالي»). Type keeps a legibility floor (line 10.5, pill 10.5 lp, pill 22 lp high): the board's proportions would give 7–8 lp at this size, so lines and pills are larger than measured — the one deliberate deviation. **One column only at a narrow width with large text** (fewer than 300 lp per two cards after text scaling, e.g. 360 lp at 1.3). Advisory diff, one grid card vs the board's first card resampled to the same size (cross_environment; different art and text by design): MAE 51.3, SSIM 0.536; the overlay puts card edges, badge, title, bar and pill row within ≈ 2 lp. *Owner on the tab row («شارة الدعم أصغر من باقي الشارات. وأزل الخط في الأسفل»):* the support badges are drawn 1.25× in the tabs (their visible height is 60–75 % of the square, the pack's 75–95 %; `KLevelArt.visualScale`), so the four look the same size; the underline under the selected tab is removed (its name stays Kabso red). Third point, completed by the owner («ولا تضيء إلا إذا كان الشخص على هذه الفئة»): in the tier grid **only the caller's current tier is lit** (full colour and a soft glow of its badge's colour); passed tiers («تم الفتح» / «مفتوح», English pill «Open») and locked ones (lock) are both dim — 65 % desaturated and 15 % darker, still legible. *Merged `origin/staging`* (D164–D168) before this: the frames contract is renumbered **v1.46-levels** and its migrations moved to `20261215090000_support_frames_enums` / `20261215100000_support_frames` (after staging's). *Support badges (owner-supplied 2026-10-30, AI-generated, `assets/levels/support/`, PROVENANCE):* two sheets sliced by alpha into 21 badges (256-px square WebP) and 21 badge-on-bar pills (96 px high); in the badge sheet rows 2–5 touch at the diamond tips (2–16 px of contact on the cut row, no body overlap) — each column was cut at its thinnest row and a 1-px neighbour sliver cleared; the old 11 support emblems are removed. **Tier mapping (default, the owner can change it):** the support ladder has 100 levels (CP-005 preset); tiers 1–20 = levels 1–5, 6–10 … 91–95, 96–99; tier 21 = level 100 alone (`KLevelArt.supportTier`). The server's `iconKey` names the 11-tier pack, so Support ignores it. *Everywhere Support shows:* the page card, the category page and its 21 tiers, and `KLevelBadge` (the level next to names: profiles, live chat, viewers, gift panel) now draws the owner's pill for the tier with the number on its bar (Latin, never mirrored) — no live file changed. *Support tier frames (owner, same day: «هذه الإطارات لكل فئة من فئات مستوى الدعم، يحصل عليها المستخدم عند وصوله فئة جديدة»; owner-supplied AI-generated sheet, 20 frames in 4 × 5 plus a separate 21st, `assets/levels/support_frames/`, PROVENANCE with each frame's photo hole):* sliced by alpha — columns by clear gaps, rows cut at their thinnest line (frames touch at the diamond tips, 7–14 px of contact, no body overlap). **Contract-first, v1.46-levels (draft, CONVENTIONS §10.22):** bag category `AVATAR_FRAME` and source `LEVEL_REWARD` (migrations `20261215090000_support_frames_enums`, `20261215100000_support_frames`: catalogue `support_frame_01…21`, `assetKey` = code); when a user's `SUPPORT` level goes up, the server gives in the same transaction the frame of every tier reached so far that they do not own (`LevelProgressService.supportFrames` → `grantLevelRewardsIn`, any path: gifts, admin adjustments, a new published version); not worn (wearing goes through the existing `PUT /me/bag/equipped`); the app's bag UI is untouched (it ignores categories it does not know; wt-bag will add frames). The category page shows each support tier's frame in the tile corner (dimmed until reached). **Owner decisions (2026-10-30):** (1) tier 21 (level 100) gets its own frame `support_frame_21` (a separate owner-supplied AI-generated image, the crimson-and-gold winged crown, trimmed and scaled like the others, its hole measured in the PROVENANCE); tiers 1–20 keep frames 1–20; (2) **levels never go down** in all four categories (owner, replacing «frames kept if the level drops»): every write keeps the higher of the stored level and what the points reach — reversals, refunds, admin deductions and a harder new version included (`max(old, new ladder)`, points unchanged); the stored `current_level` is itself the floor (no second peak column: the cleanest model, one source of truth that every reader already uses); progress counts from the kept level (0 % and the honest distance while the points are below it). **The one exception:** an admin deduction marked `fraud: true` (contract-first, v1.46-levels, CONVENTIONS §10.43; `points_ledger.fraud` with a CHECK, history reason `FRAUD`, audited, migrations `20261215120000_level_fraud_enums` / `20261215130000_level_fraud`) lowers the level to what the points reach and takes back the support frames of the tiers lost — the only way a frame is removed (a worn one leaves nothing worn). CTO default left: jumping several tiers gives every frame passed. E2E `d169-support-frames` (once per tier, replay-safe, same-tier nothing, jump gives all passed, a non-fraud deduction keeps level and frames with 0 % progress, fraud demotes and takes back the lost frames, a harder version never lowers, level 100 = 21 frames, wearable; 409 for one not owned). The rest of the contract is unchanged (`LevelSummary`/`LevelDetail` already carry everything). l10n: `levelsDetails`, `levelsHowItGrows`, `levelsTiers`, `levelsTierRange`, `levelsLv`, `levelsNeedXp`, `levelsCategories`. *Fidelity (headless Flutter render, labelled fixtures, 430 × 932 @3, light and dark; not a device capture):* the attractiveness card against the board's first card resampled to the same size (cross_environment, ADVISORY; different art, text and tint by design): MAE 63.1, SSIM 0.565 — the numbers measure the art and text, not the layout, which the overlay shows within ≈ 3 lp. 360 × 800 at text scale 1.3 checked in both languages (no overflow). Goldens re-recorded for the new look: `levels`, `level_detail`, `profile_levels` (support pill) and the screens that show a support level badge. | Owner (chat, Arabic, 2026-10-29/30, via coordinator); contract v1.46-levels draft; local commit, not pushed |

the host, a seated guest and an AUDIO listener kept the session; a video host's camera was muted in the background; **gaps:** (a) a viewer of a SOLO/MULTI live had no foreground service, so Android froze the process and the sound stopped; (b) viewers of a muted host saw a black placeholder (the «سيعود المضيف قريباً» state showed only when the host had left the media session); (c) the server had no away state: a video host could stay black for ever while heartbeats ran. *Changes:* (1) every viewer (audio and video) holds the listener service (`mediaPlayback`) — nothing ends or disconnects on background for viewer, guest or host; (2) `LiveMediaEngine.videoMuted(identity)`: the viewer screen shows the existing host-away state when the host's camera track is muted (`live_room_screen.dart`: one condition); (3) the video host's app reports `POST /live/rooms/{id}/host-presence {away}` on background/return (after muting/unmuting the camera); a failed report is resent with the next heartbeat; a 409 from either ends the live in the app; audio hosts never report; (4) **contract v1.42-live (CONVENTIONS §10.44, coordinator-assigned number; note v1.41-social also exists)**: `setLiveHostPresence` (204; 400/401/404/409/429/503 as the heartbeat), `live_rooms.host_away_since` (continuous: a repeat keeps the first time; AUDIO stores nothing), `endReason` `HOST_AWAY_TIMEOUT` (enum value in its own migration), `LIVE_HOST_AWAY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` (default 300, 30–86400); the live sweeper ends fresh SOLO/MULTI rooms away ≥ the limit in bounded `SKIP LOCKED` batches (`endedAt = host_away_since + limit`, audited `live.room.end` reason HOST_AWAY_TIMEOUT, `roomsEndedIn`, media closed by the reconciliation); stale rooms stay `HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT`. No webhook fallback: muting sends no LiveKit webhook and a killed app is already ended by the heartbeat rule in 45 s. (5) The host's notification title is «Kabso: أنت في بث مباشر» / "Kabso: You're live"; the ended screen says «انتهى البث لأن المضيف غاب أكثر من 5 دقائق.». Tests: e2e `d170-host-away` (5 min → ended; back before → not; repeat keeps the first time; MULTI too; AUDIO never; stale → HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT; non-host 404, 401, 400); app `background_d170_test` (muted host → «سيعود المضيف قريباً» AR/EN with direction, back → video; end reason; AndroidManifest / Info.plist), `live_background_test` (presence reports, retry, server end, video viewer keeps the listener service, background leaves nothing). **Needs a real phone:** Android 13/14 — viewer of a video live hears it with Instagram in front; guest on mic still heard; host video → viewers see the note, back → video; 5 min away → closed; notification tap/actions; iOS playback and mic in background (untested on a device). |

Unknown / expired / used / replaced / other-account codes get one answer (422); every refusal is audited with its reason (never the code). One second camera per live (partial unique index → 409 BUSY); none in a PK (codes and redemptions refused; a PK starting ends it — the host app at once, the server at the next media reconciliation, `end_reason` PK); the webhook and reconciliation never count `cam2:` as a viewer and remove any `cam2:` that is not the live's redeemed camera; it leaving ends it (LEFT); ending the live ends code and camera (ROOM_ENDED). Viewers and the host: the main feed full screen, the other a draggable small picture (settles on the nearer side, RTL-aware), tap = swap on that screen only; the host sets the default for everyone (`kabso.mainFeed`). PK keeps D195's split (no small picture). **(2) Music.** Files from the system document picker (Android `ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT audio/*`, no storage permission, copied to the app cache ≤ 50 MB; **no new third-party package** — a small local plugin `packages/kabso_live_music`). Choice: flutter_webrtc on Android/iOS has no API for a second audio track from a file (one capture stream per audio module), so a separate LiveKit track would need a fork; the supported hook is flutter_webrtc's **capture post-processing** (as LiveKit uses for Krisp): the plugin decodes the file (MediaExtractor/MediaCodec → mono, resampled) and mixes it **into the published microphone after echo cancelling and noise suppression**, at the music level (default 35 %), **ducked to 35 % of it while the host speaks** (60 ms attack, 600 ms release), gain ramped per frame — viewers hear voice + music in the one track; no token or contract change for the media. Player: play/pause, next, a queue of ≤ 20, volume slider, ducking switch, position. Limits (said in the sheet): the host does not hear the music on the phone; muting the mic mutes it. **Copyright:** before the first music the notice «تأكد أنك تملك حقوق تشغيل الموسيقى. يُمنع تشغيل موسيقى محمية بحقوق النشر بدون ترخيص.» — accepted once per account (stored + audited `live.music.rights_accept`); nothing plays automatically; **PRE_PRODUCTION_CHECKLIST PP-29 (music licensing for live streams, owner + legal)**. **(3) Recorder.** Option (a): on-device recording of the host's own published camera + microphone with flutter_webrtc's recorder (Android: H.264/AAC MP4 of the camera track and the microphone input); Egress (b) is not in the deploy (no egress service). Tap → «ابدأ التسجيل» → REC badge with time and a 60 s ring, stop any time, hard stop at 60 s; the room is told (`setLiveRecording` → viewers see «يتم التسجيل», clears itself after 61 s); preview → «نشر كريلز» (D162 `REEL_VIDEO` upload + `createPost reel`, caption), «حفظ في الجهاز» (MediaStore / Photos; Android 7–9 asks storage, `WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE maxSdk 28`), «تجاهل» (deleted). Limits: the clip has no second camera, no comments/gifts overlay, and **no music** (Android's recorder takes the raw microphone, before the mixer). **Honest platform states:** iOS — music and the recorder say why («…وحدة صوت خاصة بـ iOS لم تُبنَ بعد», «مسجّل WebRTC هناك لا يلتقط صوت البث»); web — not offered; the second camera works on every platform (plain LiveKit). Audio rooms: Music real, Recorder stays «قريباً» (no video). | Owner via coordinator (2026-10-31). | D210 | 2026-10-31 | **Staging bug (owner): go-live on the APK from `5217c0e` ends with «حدث خطأ غير متوقع» ~2.3 s after the media token; the app ends the room.** Status **REVIEW** (local branch `claude/kl-210-golive-fix` from `claude/kl-int6`, not pushed). *Findings:* the go-live core path is unchanged in Dart and on the server between `48d2dec` and `5217c0e` (`LiveHostController.goLive`, `LiveKitMediaEngine.publish`, token grants, webhook host branch); the failure is the `catch` of `goLive` around `LiveMediaEngine.publish` (or the steps right after it), mapped to `UnexpectedFailure` → `errorUnknown`, which ends the room (`_endQuietly`). The same build on the web with real LiveKit 1.9.1 + Chromium fake camera goes live (POST rooms 201 → token 200 → on air, heartbeats, `GET /live/hosts/{u}/targets` 200) — so it is Android-only and not reproducible here; the exception type was never shown. *Fix:* (1) `LiveMediaEngine.publish` tags its steps (`LivePublishException` connect / video / audio); `LiveStartFailure` (step + error type or server code, never a message) is kept by the controller and the go-live screen shows it in the error snack and as a persistent «تفاصيل: GL-<step>:<type>» line over the preview in every build (tap hides it). (2) Only connect + publish camera/microphone may fail going live: the heartbeat timer and the background keeper after it, the uplink stats, each room tool's start on air (gifts, PK, seat emojis, lucky, tickers, quips, program, stickers, watch, assistant) and the D201 host tools stage (music / PK watch) are guarded — a failure switches that tool off, is logged and is said once («بعض أدوات البث لم تعمل وأُوقفت. البث مستمر.»). (3) PK tick (`LivePkService.tick`): a random-queue wait whose room is no longer LIVE is deleted at once and never messaged (the «no response from servers» warning); an expired wait in a LIVE room is told once; rows are deleted before sending (no retries). | Coordinator (2026-10-31). Tests — app: new `go_live_d210_test` (12: stage wrapping, failure codes incl. no message leak, publish-failure details + room ended + hide on tap, token failure code, success clears it, background keeper throws → still live + heartbeats, a room tool throws → still live + told once, music player fails → plain camera); live folder + goldens re-run. API: d195 e2e + 1 (an ended room's wait dropped and never messaged), unit, typecheck, lint, prettier. **Needs the phone:** the «تفاصيل» code from the next go-live attempt names the Android step and exception. | |

| D215 | 2026-11-01 | **Owner bug reports, two Android phones on staging (v1.48-live DRAFT, CONVENTIONS §10.70).** Status **REVIEW** (local branch `claude/kl-215-live-bugs` from `claude/kl-int6b`, not pushed). (1) «لا يظهر صديق في بيكيه فقط بالعشوائي»: reproduced in `d215-pk-friends` e2e — two mutual followers (follows via the API) both live are each other's friend while idle, but a host waiting in the random queue (tapped «مطابقة عشوائية», or Auto Match) was left out of every candidate list and refused as `PK_HOST_BUSY`, so only the random match found them. *Fix (contract-first):* a waiting host is listed and may be invited (open invitation keeps them out of random matches; accepted ends both waits; the caller's own wait still busy). Client: the sheet's list reloads every 10 s and when it shows again; a failed read is an error with «إعادة المحاولة» and logged (was shown as «لا يوجد أصدقاء»). Friend definition unchanged: mutual follows (contract). (2) «عند الصعود بالعشوائي يخرجني من التطبيق»: no Dart/JSON fault found on the path (real `joinLivePkRandom` JSON, nulls included, through `LivePkRepositoryImpl` → controller → go-live screen in widget tests); the likely place is the native second LiveKit connection (opponent room). *Hardening:* the opponent connection is guarded per step (token / join / sound) and logged; a failed or late connection is left, never kept (it used to stay half-made and was never retried); retried on the 15 s refresh; the PK stage subtree is stable; Android audio session held while the hidden room disconnects (LiveKit stopped it for the live's own room). Native cause **unconfirmed — needs the phone** (logcat). (3) «ميوت للمايك ثم فتح لايف → تعذر»: root cause `LiveKitMediaEngine` muted the unpublished preview track (`LocalTrack.mute` stops and disposes the capture) and `publishAudioTrack` then failed (GL-publish:audio); mic off→on in setup threw (`unmute` needs a sender). *Fix:* the setup only keeps the choice; publish starts+disables the track, publishes, then LiveKit-mutes it; unmute later works; a real mic failure is still the audio step. Tests: API e2e d215 (4, new) + d195/d199 (37 with d215), kl037/kl063/d170 (60), unit 1372; app `go_live_muted_d215_test` (6), `pk_start_d215_test` (8, ar/en); `test/features/live` + `live_pk_golden_test` 1060 passed, 1 failed: `room.pk.boost` golden — its round timer is computed from the real clock, so under load its seconds flip (0.24 %, timer pill only); it passed 2 of 3 reruns with D215 and once without. `flutter analyze --fatal-infos` clean. | Owner (2026-11-01) | |

| D228 | 2026-11-01 | **Owner: «لا تستبدل لكن ضعهم لارى وان لم يعجبني نقوم بازالتهم» — a free camera effects engine (Google ML Kit + MediaPipe) BESIDE DeepAR, switchable, easy to remove.** Status **DONE (local, not pushed; not device-tested)**. *Native (plugin `kabso_camera_fx`, Android):* `FreeFxStage` runs instead of `DeepArStage` in the same `ExternalVideoFrameProcessing` chain when the engine is `free` (`CameraFxProcessor.setEngine`; DeepAR is switched off and never initialized meanwhile; the colour stage follows either). Per frame: the frame is retained and drawn on the stage's own GL thread (EGL sharing flutter_webrtc's root context) into an RGBA texture; every other frame, when the analysis thread is idle, a 256-px top-down copy is read back for **ML Kit Selfie Segmentation** (stream mode, raw 256×256 mask, EMA-smoothed, uploaded as a luminance texture) and **MediaPipe Face Landmarker** (VIDEO mode, 1 face, landmarks EMA-smoothed with jump reset and a presence fade); then one quarter-size Gaussian (2×2 separable 9-tap passes) for the background and one full-size GLSL pass: slimming pulls (4 jaw points, ≤ 3.5 % of the face width) and eye magnification (≤ 1/(1−0.16)) as an inverse warp, a 12-tap bilateral skin smoothing masked by the landmark skin ellipse (minus eyes/mouth), brighten/whiten, and the mask composite; three output textures, `glFinish` before hand-off. **Budget:** not done in 40 ms → the raw frame (the late job's output is given back to the pool, `TimedJob`); 3 slow in a row → 30 frames raw; 3 back-offs → `disabled (too_slow)`. Raw frames until what the effect needs exists (first mask; the landmarker). **No crash path:** any GL error → `error (gl_failed)` for the processor's life; init failure → `init_failed`; start watch 20 s (`no_frames` / `init_timeout` / `no_output`); face model unavailable → blur keeps running with `ready (beauty_unavailable)`, beauty alone → `error (model_unavailable)`. **Model:** `face_landmarker.task` (float16 v1, 3 758 596 B) downloaded on first beauty use from Google's storage into app files, SHA-256 pinned, `.part` + rename; not bundled. Plain-Kotlin rules in `FreeFxRules.kt` (state machine, frame budget, frame path, smoothing, `FaceShape`, `WarpMath`, `ModelFile`, `TimedJob`). Deps: `com.google.mlkit:segmentation-selfie:16.0.0-beta6`, `com.google.mediapipe:tasks-vision:1.1.1` (plugin minSdk 23 → 24; app already 24); ProGuard keep for MediaPipe. *Selector:* contract **v1.49-live** (CONVENTIONS §10.79): `LiveConfig.cameraFxEngine` `DEEPAR`|`FREE` (default DEEPAR; a `site_settings` row, migration `20270215090000_camera_fx_engine` extends the key CHECK); `getAdminCameraFx` / `updateAdminCameraFx` (`/admin/camera-fx`, OWNER via `site.settings`, audited `site.settings.update` with `key: cameraFxEngine`); admin panel card «محرك مؤثرات الكاميرا» on «إعدادات الموقع». App: `liveFxEngineProvider` (server, or the debug/profile-only override chips `srv`/`DeepAR`/`Free` under the FX diagnostics overlay, kept in prefs) → `LiveHostController.setFxEngine` → `LiveCameraFx.setEngine`. Magic: DeepAR unchanged; FREE adds the engine line «المحرك: المجاني، على الجهاز (ML Kit + MediaPipe)» and **«التجميل»** (بلا / تجميل الوجه + 4 sliders 0–100, default 40; why when the model is unavailable; the sheet scrolls); badge marks beauty. Overlay: `FREE` lines (frame avg/last vs 40 ms budget, slow/skip/backoff, analysis/seg/face ms, face, model). Viewers unchanged (the processed track). *Licences/privacy:* ML Kit Terms (proprietary, free), MediaPipe Apache-2.0, model Apache-2.0 per MediaPipe (owner/legal to confirm) — `kabso_camera_fx/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`; no key; the picture never leaves the phone, **but both SDKs send Google usage metrics** (MediaPipe `RemoteLoggingClient` / datatransport) — privacy policy / Data safety need a line if kept. No in-app native licence screen exists to update. **APK:** +36.0 MB native per arm64 (14.4 MediaPipe + 21.6 ML Kit `libxeno_native.so`, uncompressed; ~12 MB compressed), ≈ +140 MB for an all-ABI debug APK (measured from the AARs, not a build). Removal plan: KNOWN_LIMITATIONS «Removing the free engine»; DEPLOYMENT §9.2. **Follow-up (owner approved, 2026-11-01): the size increase (~12 MB per ABI compressed / ~36 MB arm64 native uncompressed) and disclosing Google's ML Kit / MediaPipe usage and performance statistics.** The privacy policy text (`infra/deploy/site/privacy.html`, ar + en) gains §2 «مؤثرات الكاميرا في البث» / “Camera effects in lives” (effects run on the device, the camera image never leaves the phone, ML Kit / MediaPipe may send Google anonymous usage and performance statistics, the face model downloads once) and a Google line in §5 — version **not** bumped (the owner publishes). Play Data safety guidance: PRE_PRODUCTION_CHECKLIST **PP-39** (PP-38 left free for parallel work); Google's pages could not be opened from here (blocked hosts), so the listed data types must be re-read against them. **Device test still pending.** **Follow-up 2 (owner approved adding DeepAR to the policy, 2026-11-01):** `privacy.html` §2 and §5 (ar + en) name DeepAR (I Love Ice Cream Ltd., UK): on-device processing, the image not sent, licence/MAU device data, and its SDK's usage-event code (Google Analytics); PP-39 extended with DeepAR's Data safety types. Verified from DeepAR's FAQ/privacy pages and the 5.6.22 AAR; DeepAR's runtime traffic not observed (KNOWN_LIMITATIONS D228). **Follow-up 3 (the D232 finding):** the live's foreground service starts a second FlutterEngine that overwrites `FlutterWebRTCPlugin.sharedSingleton` (no tracks); `KabsoCameraFxPlugin.attach` now looks the camera track up in its own engine's flutter_webrtc first, then the singleton (`OwnEngineFirst` in FrameGate.kt; `OwnEngineFirstTest` 5) — covers DeepAR, the free engine and the colour stage (one attach; EGL via process-wide `EglUtils`). Device must confirm effects still apply after the service starts. | Owner (2026-11-01), brief D228. Tests: Kotlin JVM `FreeFxRulesTest` 37 (state machine, slow-frame fallback/backoff/too_slow, GL failure → off and untouched frames, warming not counted, timed GPU job late-output release (threaded), mask/landmark smoothing, face geometry incl. roll, warp identity/fold-free/eye scale, skin weight, brighten, params clamp, analysis size/pacing, model download/checksum/size/corrupt) + the existing 43 = **80/80** (kotlinc 2.2.21 locally; CI runs `:kabso_camera_fx:testDebugUnitTest`); all plugin Kotlin compiled against the real AARs' classes; GLSL compiled in WebGL1 (SwiftShader) and the warp matched the Kotlin twin on 65 536 pixels (0 off by > 1 texel), skin weight ≤ 4/255, composite and identity exact. Plugin Dart 17/17. | D229 | 2026-11-01 | **Owner «ان يكون هناك في pk تصنيف بيكيه احكام بحيث الذي يخسر ينفذ احد ٣ احكام… اكثر من ٢٠ الف حكم», with two owner changes the same day (three levels and a server draw «قرعة»; then the final flow: the loser accepts or refuses, the winner judges).** Status **REVIEW** (local branch `claude/kl-229-pk-dares` from `claude/kl-int9` `2c4571e`, not pushed). *Contract v1.50-live (draft), CONVENTIONS §10.80 (+ §4, §7, §8, change log):* `PkType` NORMAL / DARES on `inviteLivePk`, `PkInvite`, `PkQueueWait`, `PkMatch` and the optional `joinLivePkRandom` body (a DARES waiter is paired only with a DARES waiter; Auto Match stays NORMAL); `PkMatch.dare` (`PkDare`: three `PkDareOption` EASY / MEDIUM / HARD, `drawn`, `revealAt`, `decideBy`, `endsAt`, `points`, `refusePoints`) and the event `dare`; `acceptLivePkDare`, `refuseLivePkDare`, `judgeLivePkDare`, `reportLivePkDare`; `PkHostRecord.points` / `daresDone` / `daresRefused`, `PkHistoryItem.type` / `dare`; codes `PK_DARE_CLOSED` (409), `PK_DARE_FORBIDDEN` (403); panel «أحكام PK» (`listAdminPkDares`, `createAdminPkDare`, `updateAdminPkDare`, `importAdminPkDares`, `clearAdminPkDareReports`, `getAdminPkDareStats`, `get` / `updateAdminPkDareSettings`; `pk_dares.view` OWNER / MODERATION / SUPPORT, `pk_dares.edit` OWNER / MODERATION). *Rules:* a battle ending **with a winner** draws (crypto random, audited) one of three dares, one per level, neither host has seen (per-host bitmap `pk_dare_seen`, a new cycle when a level is used up); both rooms see the same 4 s shuffle; the loser has 30 s (panel) for «قبول» / «رفض» (no answer = «رفض», owner-confirmable); «رفض» → −3 PK points at once; «قبول» → a 3-minute timer, during which only the winner judges «نُفّذ ✅» / «لم يُنفّذ ❌» (not done = −3; no verdict = done); the loser leaving (live ended / absent) = refused; the winner leaving changes nothing; a draw gives no dare; lucky gifts give 0 PK points in DARES only (still gifts for money, cherries, levels, boards). PK points: counted win +3, draw +1, loss 0, refused −3 (panel, `pk_dare_settings`), one `pk_point_events` row per match / host / kind (idempotent), audited `live.pk.points`. | D230 | 2026-11-02 | **Owner (real-device test of the staging build `claude/kl-int9` @ `2c4571e`, screenshots kept out of git, D40): «رسالة النظام لا تظهر ثم عندما احد يتحدث على التكست تظهر رسالته في الاعلى نصفها مختفي» + «الكتابة صغيرة والميداليات والمستوى صغير، واجعل الميداليات أسفل الاسم وأسفلهم الرسالة» + «في البث المباشر لا يمكن إرجاع الرسائل بإصبعي، اعمل سحب للأسفل لأرى الرسائل القديمة».** Status **REVIEW** (local branch `claude/kl-230-chat-fix` from `claude/kl-int9`, not pushed). Flutter only; no API or contract change (`GET /live/rooms/{id}/chat` returns the last 50 with no cursor, so older pages cannot be loaded: the finger scrolls through every line this visit keeps — up to 50 messages + 4 joins). *(1) Rules line (supersedes D212's positional rule):* `LiveChatState.written` is now a stored flag set by the controller only when a line ARRIVES during the visit (a text/voice/picture message from the room, this person's own pending line or media, a public message) — never from the history read on entering; joins, gifts, wins and quips do not count. The flag is kept with the visit (`LiveChatVisit.written`, plus `afterAt`), so a chat rebuilt mid-visit (minimized and reopened) remembers it. Root cause fixed: on a rebuilt visit whose last history message had gone (an expired clip/picture after 60 s, a deleted line, or older than the last 50) `indexWhere` gave −1 → split 0 → the rules line went ABOVE the whole history and the old messages counted as written → no rules line; now the split falls back to the server time of that message. The rules line is the first (newest) line on entering for host and viewer in every category. *(2) Bottom anchor + finger scroll (supersedes D199's top start and D141's whole-lines box):* `LiveChatScroll` — a reversed scroll view (newest just above the quick replies/composer, older ones rising), shrink-wrapped so a short chat sits at the bottom; the top edge fades only while there is more above (a short chat is never faded); a drag down reveals older lines; while reading older lines a new one never moves them and «رسائل جديدة ↓» (`liveChatNewMessages`) jumps back to the newest; at the newest, new lines come in at the bottom. The list takes its own taps/drags (the stage's tap-to-cheer under it never fires). The audio rooms give the chat a bounded height (no outer scroll view). Video chat area: at most 40 % / 340 pt (was 35 % / 300). The rules line still steps aside while typing in the video live (D199). *(3) Message layout (all categories):* the name on its own line (15 sp, w800, no colon), the badges row under it (20 pt: role/newcomer pills, charm 17 pt, support 16 pt, family chip, agent/recharge badges 18 pt; one row, clipped with a fade at the reading end when too many — `LiveChatFadeOverflow`), the message under them (15.5 sp, the glass shadow); the CP bubble wraps the whole block; the audio room's picture line gets the same sizes. Join, gift, lucky-win and quip lines stay one compact line at 15 sp (were 11.5–13); the rules paragraph 12 → 13 sp only (at 15 sp it filled most of the chat; deviation from the coordinator's "same base size", noted). *Tests:* new `test/features/live/live_chat_d230_test.dart` (15; the device-bug and bottom-anchor tests fail on the base code, 2/2), updated D141 §9 (stacked row), D199 → D230 bottom tests, the podcast `written` unit test, combo/cinema/moderator tests for the inner scroll; D141 §18 (whole lines) removed with `LiveChatWholeLines`. 52 goldens regenerated (go_live 14, podcast 6, room host_away/level 8, room.pk 24). Captures (scratch, not in git): `owner_cap/chat_fix/` host + viewer, 0/2/many, solo/multi/audio/podcast, ar (en one), 393×852 and 360×800 ×1.3, long name + many badges, the new-messages pill. |

| D232 | 2026-11-02 | **Owner's device test of staging (`claude/kl-int9` @ 2c4571e): «زر تابع واخرج اجعله بلوننا … يتم وضع الليفيل بجانب الكرز ازله، وازل الكرز الأول الخاص بالتكبيس الذي بجانب ترتيب الغرف … الموسيقى كل ما أضيف أغنية من جهازي يظهر هذا الخطأ»; then «وحّد الجميع واجعلهم مثل البث الحي» (the top host pill) and «بهذا الأسلوب لكن الزر أحمر وتمويج أحمر» (every small card in a live).** Status **REVIEW** (local branch `claude/kl-232-live-cleanup` from `claude/kl-int9`, not pushed). (1) New shared `KLiveDialogCard` / `showKLiveDialog` / `showKLiveConfirm` / `showKLiveInfo` (design_system): the leave prompt's floating card, a light brand-red→surface wash (dark: `surfaceRaised` with red at 22 %), picture/icon at the top centre, the primary button the theme `primary`/`onPrimary` (≥ 4.79:1 dark, ≥ 6.0:1 light), the others outlined. Converted: the leave prompt (cyan button and `#D4FDFF` wash gone), end-live confirm, kick, seat invitation, PK end confirm, PK incoming invitation, PK help (2), traffic-card buy confirm, lucky-gift 18+ confirm, target help, music-rights notice, a person's points popover, «نقاط البث» (now a card), and — inside lives only (`live: true`) — unfollow, cancel follow request and block confirms. Left as they are: large sheets (gift panel, viewers, tools grid, settings, music player), the room-info title editor (a text field), the chat image viewer, the report reason sheet and the person card's ⋯ list. (2) The chips row next to «ترتيب الغرف» (all categories, host and viewer): D135/CP-016 D level chip and D173 points chip removed (`KRoomChipsRow` keeps rank, D199/D214 target, timer, room ID); the points stay on the host pill and «نقاط البث» is in ≡ for the audio host too (viewers already had it); the host's charm level stays on the data page. (3) The audio room and podcast top bar is now the solo video live's `KHostPill` (picture, name, @username copy, cherries total / ❤ taps lines from the same sources as the video pill, the follow capsule for viewers; no room title, no level badge; it still opens the room info for the host); the row keeps the old card's bottom so the seats do not move. (4) Music — root cause: the live's foreground service (flutter_foreground_task, D123) starts a second FlutterEngine that registers every plugin again; flutter_webrtc's constructor overwrites the static `FlutterWebRTCPlugin.sharedSingleton` with that engine's instance, whose WebRTC factory never exists, so `audioProcessingController` was null and every `load` failed `not_ready` → «تعذّر تشغيل هذا الملف.». Fix: the plugin joins its own engine's flutter_webrtc (singleton only as fallback); copies go to the app's files dir under an ASCII name that keeps the extension (from the name, else the MIME type), pruned after a day; the codec is created on `load` so an unsupported format fails there (`unsupported`); `KEY_MAX_INPUT_SIZE` for WAV/FLAC; the Dart side shows a precise message per cause (`not_ready` / `unsupported` / `unreadable`), never «playing» after a decode error, and play after a failure reloads the file. Same singleton pattern exists in `kabso_camera_fx` (not changed here; flagged). Device-only: the real mix on Android with the service running, codec coverage per phone. Tests: `live_cleanup_d232_test` (19), updated go_live / host_taps / room_level / viewer_bar_d173 / live_room_d141 / host_target_d199; Kotlin `MusicFilesTest` (4, run with kotlinc on the JVM). 27 goldens regenerated (go_live.audio_*, go_live.pk.incoming.*, podcast.*, room.level.*, room.person_card.*). **Follow-up (owner, 2026-11-02): the «+» follow capsule on the top host pill is brand red** (was ref 158's cyan `#23E1DA` → `#0BD6E1`): `KHostPill.followGradient` = `color.brand.red500` `#E50914` → `red600` `#B80710`, so the white «+» is ≥ 4.79:1 on every point (the first proposal's lighter `#FF2A35` stop was 3.73:1 and was dropped). The other follow affordances in lives were already red: D231's PK opponent «+» (`#E50914`), the person card's Follow (`primary`), the ended card's «متابعة» (D221) and the leave prompt (D232). Test: `viewer_room_d131_test` checks the capsule's gradient and ≥ 4.5:1 on each stop. 77 goldens regenerated — each diff confined to the 35 × 27 lp capsule (checked per image): `room.{ended,gifts,host_away,level,multi_guest,populated}.*`, `room.state.{camera_off,reconnecting,weak}.*`, `room.pk.{battle,boost,result,split,dare.banner}.*` (ar/en × phone/android), `room.pk.activate.{double,doubleTurbo,triple,tripleTurbo}`, `room.pk.{active_gain,best_of_three,dare.draw,dare.refused}` (ar/en phone), `room.person_card.{ar,en,dark.en,frame.ar}.phone`, `room.person_card.three.ar.android`. |

| D243 | 2026-11-02 | **Owner (staging APK `c62c34c`, two Android phones): «عندما أدخل جهاز ككاميرا ثانية، الكاميرا الثانية تقف» — the D201 second camera's picture froze / stopped; also restated: the host picks the full-screen camera for everyone, each viewer swaps on their own screen.** Status **REVIEW** (local branch `claude/kl-243-cam2-freeze` from `c62c34c`, not pushed). *Root cause (the second device, `SecondCameraScreen`):* it had none of what the host's go-live has — no keep-screen-on, no foreground service, no lifecycle handling. The second phone sits untouched on a stand, its screen times out, the activity stops, and Android (9+) takes the camera from an app that is not in the foreground: the published `cam2:` track stays up with no frames (viewers and the host see a frozen last frame), and nothing restarted the camera on return. Second cause: the screen ended itself on `!engine.connected`, which is also true while LiveKit reconnects (a Wi-Fi/mobile handover): any blip showed «ended» and left the live. Third (server): a LiveKit full reconnect is a new session with the same identity whose old session's `participant_left` arrives first — `onLeft` ended the camera (`LEFT`) and the following `participant_joined` kicked it. Ruled out with evidence: dynacast/adaptive stream (both off: `lk.Room()` defaults in livekit_client 2.13.1, so no paused encodings or tiny PiP layer); the swap (flutter_webrtc 1.6.2 `setVideoTrack` is a no-op for the same track; a recreated `VideoTrackRenderer` attaches its own sink; real-LiveKit test below); flutter_webrtc's `sharedSingleton` (the second device ran no foreground service; `kabso_camera_fx` already finds its own engine first, 018b86f, so holding the service now is safe); the flip (in-place `Helper.switchCamera`, no republish). *Fix — app:* `LiveScreenAwake` (`com.kabso/screen` `keepOn`: Android `FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON` on the activity window, no permission; iOS `isIdleTimerDisabled`) on from «انضمام» until leave / ended / failure / dispose; the live foreground service held as a seated guest (type microphone; title «كاميرا ثانية · مباشر», button «مغادرة» leaves); app in the background → the camera muted the LiveKit way (the small picture goes for everyone, no frozen frame), back → unmuted (a fresh capture, the camera processor re-attached); `LiveSessionLink.sessionClosed` (set only on LiveKit's final `RoomDisconnectedEvent`, which LiveKit holds back while reconnecting) decides the end, reconnecting shows «جارٍ الاتصال…»; the engine pings on reconnecting/resuming/reconnected; a note «أبقِ هذه الشاشة مفتوحة…» on the camera screen; the on-air pill no longer overflows at large text. The small picture gets a swap icon (bottom-end, `dual.swapHint`; semantics unchanged «تبديل الكاميرتين»). *Server (CONVENTIONS §10.63 amended, no contract shape change):* `onLeft` keeps the camera while the media server still lists the identity; `onJoined` reopens a `LEFT` camera of the same identity within 60 s (`SECOND_CAMERA_REJOIN_SECONDS`) unless another camera was paired (never `REVOKED`/`EXPIRED`/`PK`). *Checked end to end in code:* the host's «الصورة الكبيرة للمشاهدين» (`setLiveSecondCameraMain` → `kabso.mainFeed` on `cam2:` → viewers' default, a viewer's own swap resets when it changes) and the viewer's tap-to-swap (this screen only). | Coordinator (2026-11-02). Tests — app: new `second_camera_d243_test` (10: screen on from join to leave, foreground service spec + stop, background pauses / return restarts / shade changes nothing, reconnect is not the end + closing is, removal, notification Leave, dispose, ar/en 360×800 ×1.3; real `LiveKitMediaEngine` with a stand-in room: reconnecting/resuming not closed, final disconnect closed; PiP swap icon + tap swap); `live_tools_d201_test`, `live_background_test`, `go_live_muted_d215_test` green (46). API: e2e `d201-live-tools` 15/15 (+1: left→joined within 60 s kept, stale left while listed kept, after 61 s refused, revoked refused); **real LiveKit 1.9.1** `d201-live-tools-livekit-real` 2/2 (+1: 30 s of cam2 frames at the viewer, ≥ 10 fps every second, while the viewer drops/takes back the track 3× and the second device reconnects as a new session with the webhooks in either order); unit 1477 passed (1 skipped); typecheck, lint, prettier clean. **Not run:** `flutter build apk` / Kotlin compile (no Android SDK or kotlinc on this machine; the Kotlin is 15 lines of `window.addFlags/clearFlags`), iOS build. **Needs the phones:** the screen staying on, the camera back after locking/unlocking, the notification on the second device. | |

| D251 | 2026-11-02 | **Owner (int11 APK, two Android phones): «الكاميرا الثانية: تشبك، لكن أوّل ما تفتح تعلق ولا تعود تستجيب» — pairing works, then the second device hangs (app/UI frozen, not just the picture) as its camera screen goes on air; and «لا تحتوي على الفلاتر، اجعلها تحتوي على الفلاتر، وعند ربط مزوّد الفلاتر تظهر عليها أيضاً».** Status **REVIEW** (local branch `claude/kl-251-cam2-hang` from `claude/kl-int12` @ `157f8d3`, not pushed; no contract, API or migration change). *Root cause (code-level; no device here — confirm with the logcat below):* flutter_webrtc 1.6.2+hotfix.4 on Android runs two **unbounded busy-waits on the platform (main) thread**: `getUserMedia` → `CameraEventsHandler.waitForCameraOpen()` (`while (state != OPENED && state != ERROR) sleep(1)`) and `trackDispose` → `removeVideoCapturer` → `stopCapture()` + `waitForCameraClosed()` (`while (state != CLOSED && state != ERROR)`). A camera that ends **DISCONNECTED** (evicted, or blocked/handed back late around a background/foreground switch or a system dialog — WebRTC's `CameraCapturer` reports `onCameraDisconnected`, and by WebRTC's Camera2Session as we know it a session already stopped by the disconnect never reports closed; the WebRTC Java sources are not in this checkout) satisfies neither, so the main thread spins for ever: input and vsync stop = the whole app hangs (ANR). D243 put the second device on exactly those paths at the moment it went on air: (a) app away/back → LiveKit `mute()`/`unmute()` = `trackDispose` / a fresh `getUserMedia` (stop + reopen of the camera) on every lifecycle change, un-debounced and unserialized (overlapping mute/unmute); (b) the live foreground service started in the same instant as the camera publish — its start asks Android 13+'s POST_NOTIFICATIONS **over the camera** (first start on a phone that never granted it) and builds a **second FlutterEngine synchronously on the main thread** (every plugin again: flutter_webrtc's `sharedSingleton` and `AudioSwitchManager.instance` replaced, D232); (c) keep-screen-on and the service were awaited/queued with no time limit (a service start that never answered blocked every later start/stop in `LiveBackgroundKeeper`). Checked and **not** the cause: FGS type `microphone` without RECORD_AUDIO (the cam2 preview opens the microphone track, so `getUserMedia` asks it before going on air; the service starts after); `com.kabso/screen` (handler already on the main thread, answers in the same call); `OwnEngineFirst` (camera_fx finds its own engine; the FGS engine's camera_fx has no Activity, attaches nothing); wakelocks/busy loops in our Dart. Also found (not changed, flagged): flutter_foreground_task 11.0.3 with `stopWithTask: true` installs `TrackVisibilityUtils`, which **stops the service on the first `onActivityPaused` with no resumed Activity** — and its resumed-set is empty when the service starts while the Activity is already resumed — so the service (host's too, D123) does not survive leaving the app; the manifest's `android:stopWithTask="true"` alone would give the intended behaviour (follow-up, device test needed). *Fix — the second camera (`SecondCameraScreen`):* every platform call is time-limited and never throws (`guardCall`: keep-screen-on 3 s, camera calls 6 s, join steps 60 s incl. permission dialogs, leave/stop/end 6 s); a failure or no answer is a note on the camera screen («تعذّر إبقاء الشاشة مضاءة…», «تعذّر تشغيل خدمة البث في الخلفية…», «الكاميرا لا تستجيب الآن…», «تعذّر تطبيق الفلتر…») — the camera and UI go on. App away/back no longer stops/reopens the camera: new `LiveSoftVideoPause.setVideoAway` on `LiveKitMediaEngine` = LiveKit `mute(stopOnMute: false)` (the others see it off) with the native track kept enabled so flutter_webrtc's own `reStartCamera` (async `startCapture`, no wait) restarts it on resume; same track id, so the filters stay attached. Away is debounced 800 ms (a dialog or quick switch touches nothing), back is immediate, one call at a time with the latest wish winning (no thrash). The foreground service starts 1.5 s after on-air, never asks the notification permission (`LiveBackgroundSpec.askNotifications: false`; it runs without it, notification hidden), a throwing `hold` is caught. `LiveBackgroundKeeper`: each start/stop holds the queue at most 30 s (a late finish re-applies what is wanted), and `hold(onUnavailable:)` tells the holder of a failed/hung start. `PlatformLiveScreenAwake` rethrows (logged) so the screen can say so. Android `com.kabso/screen`: `ScreenKeepOn` answers exactly once, at once, never throws (`no_window` / `failed`). **Diagnostics:** «نسخ سجل التشخيص» (camera screen, ended screen, after a failed join) copies `SecondCameraLog` — event names, ms, states and error **types** only (no code, token, ids, names or error text). *Filters (owner):* the cam2 camera screen has «الفلاتر» (the go-live's `showLiveFilterSheet`, same component): D143 colour filters, D228 free engine (ML Kit + MediaPipe: blur + beauty sliders) or DeepAR's effects exactly per the admin's `cameraFxEngine` (`liveFxEngineProvider` → `setFxEngine`, followed live), local to the device, «بدون فلتر» by default; a filter call that fails/hangs → back to none + note. The cam2 track already carries the `kabso_camera_fx` processor (attached at preview, before the service starts) — `OwnEngineFirst` unchanged. | Owner via coordinator (2026-11-02). Tests — app: new `second_camera_d251_test` **25** (keep-on throws/hangs, FGS throws/hangs, away/back call throws/hangs, stop+server end throw/hang, flip throws/hangs, a throwing keeper `hold`, a camera that never opens while joining, debounce + 10× thrash = no camera call, service deferred / never asks notifications / not started when leaving first, real `LiveKitMediaEngine.setVideoAway` = mute/unmute `stopOnMute: false`, no stop/restart, native kept enabled; keeper hung/throwing start; filters: none by default, sheet + colour filter reaches the camera, FREE → DeepAR switch followed, DeepAR effect when licensed, filter throws/hangs → none + note; ar/en 360×800 ×1.3 with notes; the log copied without code/token/ids/error text; log ring + `guardCall`); `second_camera_d243_test` 10 (debounce/deferral steps added), `live_tools_d201_test` (platform seams overridden), `go_live_d210_test` (keeper override signature); related suites 129/129 (d243, d251, d201, live_background, d215 muted, d228 free fx, d144b, d170, d210); the full `flutter test` once: **4061 passed, 3 skipped, 0 failed**. Kotlin JVM `ScreenKeepOnTest` **5/5** (kotlinc 2.2.21 + JUnit 4.13.2 locally; CI `:app:testDebugUnitTest`, `testImplementation junit` added). `dart format` clean, `flutter analyze --fatal-infos` clean, gitleaks (`157f8d3..HEAD`) no leaks. **Not run:** `MainActivity.kt` compile (no Android SDK here; `ScreenKeepOn.kt` compiled with kotlinc), `flutter build apk` (disk), any device test. **Needs the phones:** no hang on going on air (fresh install: no notification dialog now), lock/unlock and home/back on the second phone (picture back without a hang), filters/effects on the second camera with FREE and DeepAR, the notes. Logcat to settle the cause: `adb logcat -c`, reproduce, `adb logcat -d -v threadtime -s FlutterWebRTCPlugin:V ActivityManager:W ForegroundService:V KabsoCameraFx:V AndroidRuntime:E flutter:V` — a last `CameraEventsHandler.waitForCameraOpen`/`waitForCameraClosed` after `onCameraDisconnected` (and `ANR in com.kabso…`) confirms it. |
| D251c | 2026-11-04 | **Owner (cam2 log, phase live, DEEPAR on, «notes camera»): every flip fails in 1–6 ms with `PlatformException code=switchCamera`, no reason, picture frozen.** Status **REVIEW** (on `staging`; app + `kabso_camera_fx` Android; no contract, API or migration). *Why no reason:* flutter_webrtc's `resultError` sends `"switchCamera(): " + error` and D251b matched only messages starting with the phrase — `platformErrorTag` now cuts a leading `<name>(): ` and also keeps the native flip's `why=` and a known camera `state=` (fixed words only, ids never). *Cause (code-level, no device — the next log names it):* flutter_webrtc 1.6.2+hotfix.4 `switchCamera` (a) picks the target from ONE plugin-wide `isFacing` flag (rewritten by every getUserMedia — the HD probe — not the track's own camera), (b) needs a running camera session: a camera disconnected (evicted, a dialog, away/back) answers «camera is not running» at once and every later flip fails the same way; or (c) «Video capturer not found» (track entry / shared primary gone). *Fix:* new `CameraFlip` in `kabso_camera_fx` (channel `flipCamera`, `KabsoCameraFlipper.flip`): finds the track's capturer (primary of a shared track), the target = the other side of the camera the capturer really has; a running or opening session → WebRTC's own non-blocking `switchCamera(handler, name)`; no session → the capturer is pointed at the other camera and `startCapture` (never `stopCapture`, so no `waitForCameraClosed` busy-wait on the main thread); a switch under way → `switch-busy`; the track's entry and the plugin's `isFacing` follow. Same track: the fx stay attached, mute/away untouched. `LiveKitMediaEngine.cameraSwitch` uses it first on Android and falls back to `Helper.switchCamera` only on `why=no-capturer` or no plugin. Errors: code `cameraFlip`, `why=<no-capturer\|no-device\|switch-busy\|switch-error> [state=<NEW…FREEZED>]`. The cam2 log adds `flip-path <switch\|restart\|helper> [state=…]` after a flip. | Owner via coordinator (2026-11-04). Tests: `second_camera_d251_test` +5 (the `switchCamera(): ` prefix → `why=switch-error` / `no-capturer`, ids out; `why=`/`state=` tokens kept, unknown words dropped; `KabsoCameraFlip.fromMap`; `KabsoCameraFlipper` over a mock channel: args, refusal rethrown, null unsupported / no plugin; the log's `flip-path restart state=DISCONNECTED`); Kotlin JVM `FlipRulesTest` 4 (decision + fixed-word message; CI). **Not run here:** Kotlin compile (no Android SDK; CI's android job), any device test. **Owner:** on the second phone go live, flip 3×, lock/unlock or open a dialog, flip again — the picture follows; copy the log (`flip ok` + `flip-path …`, or `flip failed … code=cameraFlip why=… state=…`). Logcat: `adb logcat -c`, reproduce, `adb logcat -d -v threadtime -s FlutterWebRTCPlugin:V KabsoCameraFx:V CameraCapturer:V Camera2Session:V flutter:V AndroidRuntime:E`. |
