| D270 | 2026-11-04 | **Owner: «المتجر» — the Me tab's store card (D267) becomes a real store: avatar frames «إطارات الصور», profile cards «بطاقات تعريفية», chat bubbles «فقاعات الرسائل», entry banners «لافتات الدخول» and entry cars «سيارات الدخول» (more kinds later), priced in coins and / or cherries per duration (7 / 14 / 30 days) from the panel, bought for oneself or as a gift; then: the art may be an MP4 as well as an SVGA (+ PNG still).** Status **REVIEW** (on `staging`). Contract **v1.84-social (DRAFT), CONVENTIONS §10.98** (+ §4 `STORE_UNAVAILABLE`, `STORE_PRICE_CHANGED`; §7 `store:buy`; §9 row), DEPLOYMENT §5.57, migrations `20270310090000_store_enums`, `20270310090100_store`. *Decisions:* a store item **is a bag catalogue item** (`bag_items` row, code `store_<12 hex>`, its kind = its bag category) plus a `store_items` row (art, names, order, «جديد», on / off) and `store_item_prices` (one per currency × duration; a duration without a price is not offered) — so a bought item is an ordinary timed bag item: listed, worn, swept, and sendable from the bag (D171; never permanent, D266 never applies). CHAT_BUBBLE / ENTRY_BANNER / ENTRY_EFFECT are stored categories now (were VIP-only, D264); MIC_WAVE stays VIP-only; one worn item per kind extends to them (a store bubble and the VIP bubble take each other off; `UserVip.off` lists the VIP item covered). Coins: wallet ledger `STORE_PURCHASE` (guarded, `INSUFFICIENT_COINS`); cherries: cherry ledger `STORE_PURCHASE` (the withdrawal hold's guarded debit, `INSUFFICIENT_CHERRIES`; task cherries locked for withdrawal may be spent — the lock is about cashing out). One transaction serialized per buyer, `Idempotency-Key` (24 h replay / 409 on another body), audited `store.purchase`; a frozen account (coins < 0) buys nothing in either currency (403 `TOPUP_ACCOUNT_FROZEN`). Buying a held item adds the days to its end (≤ 3 650 days ahead). A gift (`recipient` username) lands as `GIFT` with `giftedBy` and the bag's `BAG_ITEM_RECEIVED` notification; 404 for a blocked / inactive / unknown account, 400 for oneself, `ACCOUNT_RESTRICTED` under «منع إرسال الهدايا». No economy conversion in the server: the panel sets each price (the coin / cherry dollar values are shown as a hint). *Renderers:* worn store items travel as `UserSummary.wear` / `PublicProfile.wear` (`StoreWear`: art per kind; a store frame / card never in `avatarFrame` / `profileCard`, which stay the bundled ones; the room's host summary too); the app folds `wear` into `UserVip` (a store-only look when no VIP: no badge, no name colour) and the look puts each store item over the VIP perk of its kind — so the frame (seats, person card, profile), the person card's art, the chat bubble (MP4 stretched; SVGA 9-sliced) and the entry banner / car (a join wearing one is never dropped by the 1 / s window) all reuse the VIP renderers. MP4 art plays on the gifts' D207 path (`ArtVideoPlayer`: gift art cache file, gift video controller, the side-by-side alpha shader; the still with reduced motion, in tests and where the shader cannot run). *Panel:* «المتجر» (Programs → المتجر, `/store`; `catalog.view` reads, `catalog.manage` OWNER writes): tabs by kind, the items with art, prices, «جديد», owners and sales count; add / edit (kind fixed after adding) with PNG + SVGA / MP4 uploads and previews, the MP4 transparency, a 2 × 3 price grid; on / off; each with a reason, audited `store.item.*`; the latest sales with totals. *App:* the Me hub card opens `/store`: balances, tabs by kind, a 3-column grid (preview, name, «جديد», «تملكه», lowest price coins first); the item sheet: a big preview (the frame around the person's own picture, the card behind their name, the bubble around sample text, the banner with «دخل … البث», the car), duration chips and a currency toggle (only those priced), price and balance, «شراء» (Kabso red) / «إهداء» (a username or someone followed), short coins → «شحن» (the wallet), short cherries explained, «ارتدِ الآن» after buying; the bag shows store art; the coin and cherry histories name «شراء من المتجر». Tests: API unit `store-rules.spec` (+ `bag.catalogue.spec`, `vip-bag.spec`, `error-mapping.spec` updated); e2e `d270-store` (panel uploads SVGA / MP4 / still, add / edit / switch / audit, SUPPORT read-only; buy with coins, with cherries, extend, insufficient coins / cherries, unpriced / changed price / disabled item, Idempotency-Key replay and conflict, gift with notification, blocked / suspended / self recipient, frozen account, worn store bubble vs VIP bubble in `wear` / `off`, sales totals) + D264 / D171 / D252 / D258 / D265 / D169 / D186 / D197 / D179 / D184 / D185 / D208 / D164 suites; admin vitest `store.test`; Flutter `store_d270_test` (grid, sheet, buy, wear now, recharge, gift, price changed, `wear` parsing and the look) + goldens `store_grid`, `store_item_sheet` (ar / en, 393 × 852, insets 59 / 34); `me_hub_screen_test` (the store opens). | IMPLEMENTED (draft; not deployed, not device-tested) |

| D262 | 2026-11-04 | **Owner (phone streamer live): «لم تسمح بتصوير الشاشة، لذلك لم يبدأ البث» at once on «ابدأ», and the Android screen-capture dialog never appeared.** Status **REVIEW** (on `staging`; app + `kabso_streamer` Android + CI step; no contract, API or migration). *Cause (code-level, no device — the logcat below settles it):* the consent was flutter_webrtc 1.6.2+hotfix.4's `requestCapturePermission`, which adds a deprecated framework `android.app.Fragment` to the Activity and calls `startActivityForResult` from it (re-asked on every `onResume`, a swallowed commit error, no answer at all without an Activity); any answer that was not OK, and any exception, became `false` in Dart — so a dialog the system closed at once (or never started) read as «you refused». *Fix:* `kabso_streamer` asks itself: `requestCapture {fullScreenOnly}` from the attached Activity with `ActivityPluginBinding.addActivityResultListener` and `MediaProjectionManager.createScreenCaptureIntent()` (Android 14+ `createConfigForDefaultDisplay()` when full screen only; off — same choice as before). Granted → the result Intent goes into this engine's flutter_webrtc `GetUserMediaImpl.mediaProjectionData` (reflection along `methodCallHandler` → `getUserMediaImpl`, own engine first, D232; kept by flutter_webrtc's ProGuard rules), so livekit's `getDisplayMedia` uses it and never asks again (Android 14: one consent, one projection). Answers `{outcome: granted\|refused\|failed, reason}` — reasons `no_activity`, `no_service`, `no_dialog`, `security`, `error`, `superseded`, `activity_gone`, `no_data`, `no_webrtc`, and `instant_cancel` (cancelled < 400 ms after asking: the dialog never showed). Dart: `StreamerConsent` (granted / refused / failed + short code; platform errors `PlatformException.code`, `no_plugin`, `timeout` after 3 min), logged without ids (`streamer: consent failed(instant_cancel)`); the host sees «لم تسمح بتصوير الشاشة…» only for a «no», otherwise «تعذّر طلب إذن تصوير الشاشة (code)»; «شارك الشاشة من جديد» that cannot start says «تعذّر تشغيل مشاركة الشاشة، حاول مرة أخرى» (was «you refused» too). D213 order unchanged: consent → service gains `mediaProjection` → room → screen. | Owner via coordinator (2026-11-04). Tests: new `streamer_consent_d262_test` 8 (parse, code, the channel: method/args, refused, PlatformException code, MissingPlugin, messages ar/en); `streamer_d213_test` +3 (failed consent → its own message, nothing created; a throwing consent; share again: «no» / failed / could not start). Kotlin JVM `StreamerRulesTest` +3 (hand-over into a private field incl. superclass, wrong shape refused, outcomes incl. `instant_cancel`); CI now runs `:kabso_streamer:testDebugUnitTest`. Full `flutter test` and `flutter analyze` green. **Not run here:** Kotlin compile (no Android SDK; CI's android job), any device. **Owner:** start a streamer live — the system dialog «بدء التسجيل أو البث؟» shows; «إلغاء» → «لم تسمح…»; «ابدأ الآن» → the live starts with the screen; any other message names the code. Logcat: `adb logcat -c`, reproduce, `adb logcat -d -v threadtime -s KabsoStreamer:V FlutterWebRTCPlugin:V ActivityTaskManager:I ActivityManager:W flutter:V AndroidRuntime:E` — `KabsoStreamer: consent: asked` then `consent: <outcome> (<reason>), code <n> after <ms>ms`. |
| D262b | 2026-11-04 | **Owner (device test of D262): the Android dialog now shows, but on Android 14+ it starts on «تطبيق واحد»; «بدء» then back out of the app picker reads as refused — ask for the entire screen directly.** Status **REVIEW** (on `staging`; app + `kabso_streamer` Android; no contract, API or migration). *Change:* the app asks `requestCapture {fullScreenOnly: true}` (the native default too); on Android 14+ (`StreamerRules.wholeDisplayConsent`: SDK ≥ 34) the intent is `createScreenCaptureIntent(MediaProjectionConfig.createConfigForDefaultDisplay())` — the dialog offers only the entire screen, no single-app choice or app picker; below 34 the plain intent (already the whole screen). flutter_webrtc 1.6.2+hotfix.4 is unaffected by the config: `OrientationAwareScreenCapturer` calls `getMediaProjection(RESULT_OK, data)` with the handed-over `mediaProjectionData`, registers its callback, then a virtual display of the default display — the same path flutter_webrtc's own `fullScreenOnly` uses. A cancel still says «لم تسمح بتصوير الشاشة…». New one-line hint in the go-live streamer panel above the privacy note: «اضغط "بدء" في نافذة أندرويد لمشاركة شاشتك» (`GoLiveStreamerPanel.consentHintKey`). | Owner via coordinator (2026-11-04). Tests: `streamer_consent_d262_test` (args now `fullScreenOnly: true`), `streamer_d213_test` +1 (the hint in the setup); Kotlin JVM `StreamerRulesTest` +1 (`wholeDisplayConsent`: 34/36 yes, 33/23 no, not asked → no); CI step `:kabso_streamer:testDebugUnitTest` kept. Full `flutter analyze`, `dart format`, `flutter test` green. **Not run here:** Kotlin compile, any device. **Owner:** on Android 14+ the dialog shows «الشاشة بالكامل» only → «بدء» → live; «إلغاء» → «لم تسمح…». |
| D261 | 2026-11-04 | **Owner (device): «في بث البودكاست رسائل المشاهد لا تظهر عند المضيف»** — the viewer's line showed on the viewer's phone (not «failed»), not on the podcast host's. Status **REVIEW** (on `staging`, app only). *Investigation:* every link is mode-agnostic and works in tests — the viewer's podcast composer posts `POST …/chat` (new `podcast_d212_test` case), the API stores and sends every room's lines the same way (`broadcastData(row.id, 'chat')`, no PODCAST rule, no «questions» diversion), the host's engine parses any `chat` data message, the host attaches the chat in every mode and the podcast host's compact chat draws a received line on screen inside the chat list (new `podcast_host_chat_d261_test`, 360 × 800 ar). No podcast-only defect was found in code; the one way a stored line stays off the host's screen is a **lost data message** — chat delivery is best effort (§10.11: an error on the media server is logged and dropped, a reconnect or a phone put down while talking misses it) and nothing ever read it again. *Fix:* `LiveChatController.catchUp()` re-reads `GET …/chat` and adds, in order, each message this chat never had (ids it read from the history, data messages and its own sends are remembered, so a line is never doubled and a deleted one never comes back); the host's live calls it after each heartbeat (15 s, and on return from the background), so a viewer's line reaches the host at the latest one heartbeat later. Viewers are unchanged (no extra reads). Tests: `podcast_host_chat_d261_test` (a data message shows on the podcast host; a line whose data message never came shows at the next heartbeat once and in order, a deleted one stays gone — fails without the fix), `podcast_d212_test` (+1: the listener's line is sent). **Not verified:** a device / real LiveKit (whether the owner's lines were lost in delivery; the API logs «Could not send a chat data message to the media room» would say). |

| D272 | 2026-11-04 | **Owner (device): «مؤثر الدخول يتوقف يعلق ولا يحتوي على صوت لماذا ؟» — the VIP entry car stutters / freezes and is silent.** Status **REVIEW** (on `staging`). CONVENTIONS **§10.94.7** (+ §10.94.8). App only; no contract change. *The art:* `peacock_000.svga` 13.8 MB (zlib, SVGA 2.1.0): 75 sprites, each a full-canvas 563 × 1000 PNG (one per frame), 12 fps (6.25 s), viewBox 563 × 1000, + one MP3 in `audios` (`peacock`, frames 0–75, `totalTime` 6754 ms, 108 KB). *Causes:* (1) `SVGAParser.decodeFromBuffer` on the UI isolate inflated + parsed the 13.8 MB and decoded every picture **full size** (42 M px ≈ 169 MB); (2) `VipSvgaCache.render` then drew all 75 frames at once with `toImageSync` (44 M px ≈ 177 MB at the car's 1024 bucket, ≈ 350 MB peak) — over the whole 24 M-px cache, evicting every chat badge / bubble / frame, which were then decoded again; (3) the 10 s car limit counted from the `join`, so download + decode cut the car mid-way («يتوقف»); (4) the engine kept only the frames: the parser's `audiosData` was dropped with the movie. *Fix (`vip_svga.dart`):* `VipSvgaDoc.read` inflates and parses on **another isolate** (`compute`) into plain data (encoded pictures, per-sprite frame floats, frame → pictures, max drawn size, sounds); pictures decoded by the engine (`ImageDescriptor.instantiateCodec`) at the size they are drawn, never bigger; frames drawn one at a time with async `toImage`; a clip whose frames exceed `clipPixels` (12 M) is **live**: a per-playback `VipSvgaReader` decodes ≈ 1 s ahead one picture at a time and releases the ones behind, the painter draws the frame from it; a doc LRU (3) keeps the next entrance of the same tier instant. *Entrance:* art warmed as the `join` is queued (and the host's own); the car starts **only when ready** (`carWait` 8 s else skipped, file stays read) then lasts its own length + 1 s (≤ 10 s); a late picture holds the last frame, the clock goes on. *Sound:* `VipSvga.sound` (car; the banner's only when there is no car) plays `MovieEntity.audios` through the gifts' `GiftEffectSound` (media, no audio focus, iOS session untouched; `GiftSoundClip.startAt` = SVGA `startTime`, also for gifts), only with «المؤثرات الصوتية» on and in the foreground, one entrance at a time (`VipSvgaAudio`), stopped when the car ends / is removed / the app leaves the foreground; reduced motion: still and **no sound**; silent switch not read (as the gifts). Chat badges, frames, bubbles, cards, mic waves never sound. *Gifts:* already play their SVGA's sound (D126/D207) — no gap; but `KGiftEffect` still parses with `SVGAParser` on the UI isolate with full-size bitmaps, so a 13 MB gift SVGA would hitch the same way — reported, not changed (different player). | Owner (2026-11-04). *Bench* (`test/features/vip/vip_entry_bench_d272_test.dart`, `VIP_ENTRY_SVGA=<file>`, flutter test on the dev box, software raster): **before** decode 1.1–1.2 s with the UI isolate stalled 0.76–0.88 s, render 0.13–0.29 s UI stall + raster 0.4–1.4 s, 177 MB of frames + 169 MB of bitmaps, no sound; **after** read 0.13–0.73 s on another isolate (UI stall 36–263 ms, typ. ≈ 45 ms), clip 1–55 ms, first second decoded in 82–217 ms (UI stall 8–40 ms), played at 12 fps with 0 / 75 frames late, ≤ 14 pictures held (≈ 32 MB), per-frame paint the same order (one picture per frame either way; software-raster numbers noisy, 40–136 ms both). Tests: new `vip_entry_sound_d272_test.dart` 9 (parse: sounds' start / end frame, startTime, totalTime, end-less sound → whole clip, pictures apart from MP3, frame → picture, drawn size; decode size never bigger; live clip + reader window and release; small clip keeps frames; sound with a fake player: starts at frame 0 / frame 2 with the 120 ms offset, stops at the end with `onDone`, stops when removed, a second entrance stops the first, silent with sound off / reduced motion / background, stops when the app leaves the foreground); existing VIP, gift and bag tests pass; full `flutter test` 4241 passed / 12 skipped, `flutter analyze --fatal-infos` and `dart format --set-exit-if-changed lib test` clean. App only; no contract change. *Cause:* minimizing the live disposes its screen and the `LiveVipEntranceLayer`; opening it again builds a new layer, and D265's host entrance (`self:`) played on every layer start ("or the host comes back to it"). Its join memory (`_seen`, a chat serial) also lived in the widget and the chat's serials restart when the chat is rebuilt. *Fix:* `VipEntrancePlays` (`vipEntrancePlaysProvider`, kept while the app runs, by room): the host's own entrance once per room (each go-live is a new room); the chat's newest join is handled once by identity (a layer built again marks the join already in the chat as handled, so a backlog never plays); the same person's entrance again within 60 s (`rejoinQuiet`: their media reconnecting) plays nothing. Leaving the room (`LiveRoomScreen` closed without minimizing, or ✕ on the floating window) `forget`s it, so the next visit plays entrances again. Checked: gift effects, the gift/lucky/level tickers, the CP and agent entrances read only live data messages (no replay; the providers are rebuilt with the screen). *Car at the bottom:* the car fills the layer (the whole screen, under the bars) with `BoxFit.fitWidth` + `Alignment.bottomCenter`: full width, its bottom on the screen's bottom edge, a taller art loses its top; `SvgaDoc.area` (the clip's drawn area inside its canvas) trims empty canvas rows below the art (`VipSvga.trimBottom`); the MP4 car (`ArtVideoPlayer.alignment`) and the still the same. The banner keeps its place. *Tests:* `vip_entrance_d280_test.dart` — join plays once across minimize/restore, no backlog, minimized mid-play, reconnect within a minute, the host's `self` once per live and again after leaving; the car's pixels on a 393 × 852 screen (fixture with empty rows below, a taller car); the owner's peacock with `VIP_ENTRY_SVGA` (preview only, not committed). | D300 | 2026-11-07 | **Owner: a medal «مدرسة المحتوى» / Content School with 4 levels, each with the owner's own art (`437`–`440.webp`), reached when the content creator has ALL THREE of its followers, likes and views — L1 2 000 / 10 000 / 20 000 · L2 10 000 / 50 000 / 100 000 · L3 100 000 / 200 000 / 500 000 · L4 1 000 000 / 2 000 000 / 5 000 000.** Status **IMPLEMENTED** (uncommitted, branch `claude/kl-300-content-school-medal` off the int16 head 4f2a77f; awaiting the owner's review — not deployed, not device-tested). Contract **v1.83-live** (draft, additive; the medals series), CONVENTIONS **§10.111** (+ §8 and §9 rows), DEPLOYMENT **§5.79**, **PP-57**, migrations `20270401090000_medal_requirement_metrics` (the enum values alone), `20270401090100_medal_content_school`. **Interpretations owner-confirmed 2026-11-07** (via the coordinator): (1) content creators only (`creator_members`); a non-creator never earns or progresses; a level already earned is kept if the membership ends (D291: medals never go down); (2) current ACTIVE followers (as the profile shows), plus all-time likes and views from other ACTIVE accounts, never self; (3) views and likes count posts AND reels — **including posts later deleted or removed by moderation** (owner answer 2026-11-07: «لا» to leaving them out). *Design (CTO):* D291's levels model extended minimally — `medal_level_requirements(medal_code, level, metric, threshold)` rows per level; the medal's metric `ALL_REQUIREMENTS` whose value is the level the requirements reach (each level's threshold = its number), so `raiseMedalLevelsIn` (now optionally per medal code) raises it unchanged; single-metric medals untouched. Views from ACTIVE accounts need to know who viewed: new `post_views.counted_views` (rows before the migration start at 1 — a lower bound). *Recompute (CTO):* follows, likes and views are hot paths and the exact counts are O(followers + likes) per creator, so **a sweeper** (`MEDAL_CREATOR_SWEEP_INTERVAL_SECONDS`, default 600, refused 0 in production) with an upper-bound pre-filter (all follows, the posts' `like_count` / `view_count` sums) counts exactly only creators near their next level; plus at once on a creator's activation, and `medals-admin backfill` for history. *App:* the card's bar = the lowest of the three; the sheet shows three bars and each level's three thresholds; «لصنّاع المحتوى فقط» for non-creators; current level's art on the stage, card, profile and picker; no number on the art. *Panel:* «الميداليات» edits the three thresholds per level. Descriptions «لصنّاع المحتوى: المتابعون والإعجابات والمشاهدات معاً.» / "For content creators: followers, likes and views together." Art: `assets/medals/medal_content_school_l1` … `_l4.webp` (512², alpha; PROVENANCE). **Second medal in D300 (owner, same day): «لاعب الأحكام» / Dares Player** — «عندما يكون نقاط PK تحدي = 1500 / 3000 / 5000 / 20000» (the owner's «المستوى الأول» on the last two lines read as levels 3 and 4): single-metric `PK_DARES_POINTS` = the host's **«PK أحكام» (DARES) points only** (owner answer 2026-11-07), kept in the new `pk_host_records.dares_points` (the DARES matches' share of `PkHostRecord.points`, D229 / D276; the same once-per-match `pk_point_events`), L1 ≥ 1 500, L2 ≥ 3 000, L3 ≥ 5 000, L4 ≥ 20 000; raised in `addPkPointsIn`'s transaction (the only writer of the points), never lowered when the total falls (a refused dare); anyone with a PK record (no creator gate); tab ACHIEVEMENT with the other medals (owner: «هذه ميداليات»; Content School too); art `medal_dares_player_l1` … `_l4` (owner's `441`–`444.webp`); «نقاط PK التي جمعتها في التحديات.» / "PK points you earned in battles."; migrations `20270401090200_medal_dares_player`, `20270401090300_medal_dares_only_points` (`PK_POINTS` renamed `PK_DARES_POINTS`, `dares_points` filled from history, both medals to ACHIEVEMENT). **Owner's answers 2026-11-07 (decided):** DARES only; views before the migration counted once per viewer (a lower bound) accepted; deleted / removed posts' likes and views count; both medals in the other medals' tab (CONVENTIONS §10.111.8). Status: the follow-up is IMPLEMENTED uncommitted on top of df88ffc. |
- **Storage:** Cloudflare R2. No account is created and nothing is activated until the owner supplies credentials and approves the cost.
- **Moderation:** no provider yet, so uploads stay visible to their owner only (`PENDING`); `allow_all` stays dev-only.
- **Country names:** CLDR 48 names unchanged (including PS). XK excluded by default.

CTO decisions:
- Accept the extra tightening of inner spaces (SEC-039 family).
- Accept the IVD 2022-09-13 pin; KL-049 verifies it against the latest published release.
- Image processing runs in a bounded, sandboxed path; A7 reviews it.
- Merge the contract only together with its implementation | Uploads stay off in production until provider credentials, a moderation decision and an A7 review exist |
1. Add avatar and room-cover uploads. The storage provider is to be chosen; an S3-compatible adapter is built and stays disabled until the owner approves the provider, cost and credentials.
2. Start KL-034, the live room redesign, following the reference's patterns: full-bleed video, host pill with follow, viewer avatars, chat, gifts.
3. Add a Countries & Regions discovery section. The user chooses their country (optional); location is never inferred.
4. Keep Kabso's red identity; no teal/purple palette | Feature freeze lifted for these items only |
- **SEC-036:** property-based hygiene (Unicode 17 vendored, sha256-pinned). Tightening applies on write only.
- **SEC-035:** reads filter stale rooms and do no unbounded writes; the sweeper ends them in bounded batches. Supersedes D42 item 4.
- **§7:** the replace/both/default rate-limit table is written out.
- **D41:** the heartbeat 503 names `LIVE_HOSTING_UNAVAILABLE`.
- **QA-001:** a malformed or oversized JSON body is rejected before auth on every route (documented, no behaviour change).
- **QA-002:** an existing follower re-PUTting to a private account gets 200 (code change).

CTO choices:
- Sweeper batch size becomes the named setting `LIVE_SWEEP_BATCH_SIZE` (default 500, bounded).
- The `unsupported_encoding` / `unsupported_charset` issue values the API already emits are added to the §4 vocabulary in the next patch.
- Accepted side effect: U+0600–U+0605 and U+06DD (format characters) are rejected in titles; recitation titles can use U+FD3E/U+FD3F and digits | KL-045 implements; A7 re-verifies |
1. `LIVE_CURSOR_KEY` is optional. When unset it is derived by HKDF (`live-cursor/v1`) from `RATE_LIMIT_KEY_SECRET`, matching the `REGISTRATION_CODE_KEY` pattern. When set it is strength-checked in production.
2. Rate limits: v1.3 routes follow CONVENTIONS §7 (the endpoint bucket replaces the per-user default). Older routes still count both buckets, which is stricter and therefore safe. §7 wording is clarified in the next contract patch.
3. Acknowledged a second read-only cross-module exception: the room list reads `users`/`user_profiles` for the ACTIVE-host filter and the host summary.
4. The per-request stale sweep (one UPDATE) is accepted for v1.3. Its cost is measured in the PP-32 load test.
5. `AccountLifecycleService.suspend/softDelete` has no HTTP route yet. The future admin work and KL-014 must call it; ops procedures must not bypass it. | Contract-conformant; no contract change needed for 1/3/4 |
